// الكسر الصحيح يحتاج إغلاقا واضحا خارج المستوى، لا مجرد ذيل. مكان الإغلاق
// وقوة الجسم يقللان كسور الافتتاح الضعيفة، بينما يبقى الحجم/VWAP/FVG حسب الإعدادات.
bool bullishBreakCandleQuality = closeLocation >= 0.55 and (bodyRatio >= 0.30 or vsaWideResult)
bool bearishBreakCandleQuality = closeLocation <= 0.45 and (bodyRatio >= 0.30 or vsaWideResult)
bool bullishChannelBreakSetup = qualifiedChannelBreakUpEvent and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and vwapLongPass and fvgBullPass
bool bearishChannelBreakSetup = qualifiedChannelBreakDownEvent and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and vwapShortPass and fvgBearPass
bool bullishMajorChannelBreakSetup = majorBreakoutUpEvent and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and vwapLongPass and fvgBullPass
bool bearishMajorChannelBreakSetup = majorBreakoutDownEvent and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and vwapShortPass and fvgBearPass
bool swingLevelsCompatible = na(lastSwingHigh) or na(lastSwingLow) or lastSwingHigh > lastSwingLow + syminfo.mintick
bool bullishBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not highWasBroken and close > lastSwingHigh + breakBuffer and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and vwapLongPass and fvgBullPass
bool bearishBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingLow) and not lowWasBroken and close < lastSwingLow - breakBuffer and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and vwapShortPass and fvgBearPass
bool bullishHorizontalBreakSetup = strongResistanceBreak and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and vwapLongPass and fvgBullPass
bool bearishHorizontalBreakSetup = strongSupportBreak and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass and vwapShortPass and fvgBearPass

bool bullishSweep = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingLow) and not lowWasBroken and not lowWasSwept and low < lastSwingLow - breakBuffer and close > lastSwingLow
bool bearishSweep = barstate.isconfirmed and swingLevelsCompatible and not na(lastSwingHigh) and not highWasBroken and not highWasSwept and high > lastSwingHigh + breakBuffer and close < lastSwingHigh
bool twoSidedLiquiditySweep = bullishSweep and bearishSweep
bool bullishLiquiditySweep = bullishSweep and not bearishSweep
bool bearishLiquiditySweep = bearishSweep and not bullishSweep

var int structureBias = 0
var int lastStructureBreakBar = na
if structureBias == 0 and dowBias != 0
    structureBias := dowBias

int structureBreakGraceBars = math.max(2, pivotBars * 2 + 1)
bool recentStructureBreakBeforeCurrent = not na(lastStructureBreakBar) and bar_index - lastStructureBreakBar <= structureBreakGraceBars
bool structureConflictBeforeBreak = not recentStructureBreakBeforeCurrent and structureBias != 0 and dowBias != 0 and structureBias != dowBias
int structureBiasBeforeBreak = not swingLevelsCompatible ? 0 : structureConflictBeforeBreak ? dowBias : structureBias != 0 ? structureBias : dowBias
bool bullishChoch = bullishBreak and structureBiasBeforeBreak == -1
bool bearishChoch = bearishBreak and structureBiasBeforeBreak == 1
bool bullishBos = bullishBreak and not bullishChoch
bool bearishBos = bearishBreak and not bearishChoch

if bullishBreak
    highWasBroken := true
    structureBias := 1
    lastStructureBreakBar := bar_index
else if bearishBreak
    lowWasBroken := true
    structureBias := -1
    lastStructureBreakBar := bar_index

// A close-confirmed break bias and the latest Dow pivot sequence can disagree
// temporarily. Treat that state as a conflict rather than awarding both sides
// a structural point or presenting one of them as the sole market direction.
bool recentStructureBreak = not na(lastStructureBreakBar) and bar_index - lastStructureBreakBar <= structureBreakGraceBars
bool structureDirectionConflict = not recentStructureBreak and structureBias != 0 and dowBias != 0 and structureBias != dowBias
int resolvedStructureBias = not swingLevelsCompatible or structureDirectionConflict ? 0 : structureBias != 0 ? structureBias : dowBias

bool doubleBottomConfirmed = bullishBreak and doubleBottomArmed and not na(doubleBottomNeckline) and math.abs(lastSwingHigh - doubleBottomNeckline) <= patternTolerance
bool doubleTopConfirmed = bearishBreak and doubleTopArmed and not na(doubleTopNeckline) and math.abs(lastSwingLow - doubleTopNeckline) <= patternTolerance
float bullishMeasuredTarget = doubleBottomConfirmed ? doubleBottomNeckline + doubleBottomHeight : na
float bearishMeasuredTarget = doubleTopConfirmed ? doubleTopNeckline - doubleTopHeight : na

if doubleBottomConfirmed or (doubleBottomArmed and not na(doubleBottomPrice) and close < doubleBottomPrice - breakBuffer)
    doubleBottomArmed := false
    doubleBottomArmedBar := na
    doubleBottomNeckline := na
    doubleBottomPrice := na
    doubleBottomHeight := na
if doubleTopConfirmed or (doubleTopArmed and not na(doubleTopPrice) and close > doubleTopPrice + breakBuffer)
    doubleTopArmed := false
    doubleTopArmedBar := na
    doubleTopNeckline := na
    doubleTopPrice := na
    doubleTopHeight := na

// A zone now requires both a close-confirmed structure break and a displacement
// candle. Its base follows proximal/distal rules from the supplied references.
// Only one subtle box per side is retained, so the chart stays readable.
bool bullishZoneDisplacement = bullishBreak and bodyRatio >= zoneImpulseBodyRatio and closeLocation >= 0.65
bool bearishZoneDisplacement = bearishBreak and bodyRatio >= zoneImpulseBodyRatio and closeLocation <= 0.35
[demandOriginReady, demandFarthestOffset, demandProximalCandidate, demandDistalCandidate, demandBaseCandidateCount, demandPatternCandidate] = f_zone_origin(true, zoneLookback, zoneBaseBodyRatio)
[supplyOriginReady, supplyFarthestOffset, supplyProximalCandidate, supplyDistalCandidate, supplyBaseCandidateCount, supplyPatternCandidate] = f_zone_origin(false, zoneLookback, zoneBaseBodyRatio)
var bool demandZoneActive = false
var bool supplyZoneActive = false
var bool demandZoneRetested = false
var bool supplyZoneRetested = false
var float demandZoneHigh = na
var float demandZoneLow = na
var float supplyZoneHigh = na
var float supplyZoneLow = na
var int demandZoneCreatedBar = na
var int supplyZoneCreatedBar = na
var int demandZoneBaseCount = 0
var int supplyZoneBaseCount = 0
var int demandZonePatternCode = 0
var int supplyZonePatternCode = 0
var box demandZoneBox = na
var box supplyZoneBox = na

bool validDemandCandidate = demandOriginReady and not na(demandProximalCandidate) and not na(demandDistalCandidate) and demandProximalCandidate > demandDistalCandidate
bool validSupplyCandidate = supplyOriginReady and not na(supplyProximalCandidate) and not na(supplyDistalCandidate) and supplyDistalCandidate > supplyProximalCandidate

if bullishZoneDisplacement and validDemandCandidate
    demandZoneActive := true
    demandZoneRetested := false
    demandZoneHigh := demandProximalCandidate
    demandZoneLow := demandDistalCandidate
    demandZoneCreatedBar := bar_index
    demandZoneBaseCount := demandBaseCandidateCount
    demandZonePatternCode := demandPatternCandidate
    if not na(demandZoneBox)
        box.delete(demandZoneBox)
    demandZoneBox := stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones ? box.new(left = bar_index - demandFarthestOffset, top = demandZoneHigh, right = bar_index + visualPlanBoxOffsetBars, bottom = demandZoneLow, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, border_color = color.new(bullColor, zoneBorderTransparency), border_width = 1, bgcolor = color.new(bullColor, zoneFillTransparency), text = "منطقة طلب", text_color = color.new(bullColor, 8), text_size = size.tiny, text_halign = text.align_right, text_valign = text.align_center) : na

if bearishZoneDisplacement and validSupplyCandidate
    supplyZoneActive := true
    supplyZoneRetested := false
    supplyZoneHigh := supplyDistalCandidate
    supplyZoneLow := supplyProximalCandidate
    supplyZoneCreatedBar := bar_index
    supplyZoneBaseCount := supplyBaseCandidateCount
    supplyZonePatternCode := supplyPatternCandidate
    if not na(supplyZoneBox)
        box.delete(supplyZoneBox)
    supplyZoneBox := stockChartAllowed and showSupplyDemandZones ? box.new(left = bar_index - supplyFarthestOffset, top = supplyZoneHigh, right = bar_index + visualPlanBoxOffsetBars, bottom = supplyZoneLow, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, border_color = color.new(bearColor, zoneBorderTransparency), border_width = 1, bgcolor = color.new(bearColor, zoneFillTransparency), text = "منطقة عرض", text_color = color.new(bearColor, 8), text_size = size.tiny, text_halign = text.align_right, text_valign = text.align_center) : na

bool demandZoneInvalidated = barstate.isconfirmed and demandZoneActive and close < demandZoneLow - breakBuffer
bool supplyZoneInvalidated = barstate.isconfirmed and supplyZoneActive and close > supplyZoneHigh + breakBuffer
bool demandZoneExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and demandZoneActive and not na(demandZoneCreatedBar) and bar_index - demandZoneCreatedBar > zoneMaxBars
bool supplyZoneExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and supplyZoneActive and not na(supplyZoneCreatedBar) and bar_index - supplyZoneCreatedBar > zoneMaxBars
bool bullishDemandRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and demandZoneActive and not demandZoneRetested and not demandZoneInvalidated and not demandZoneExpired and bar_index > demandZoneCreatedBar and low <= demandZoneHigh and high >= demandZoneLow and close > demandZoneHigh and close > open
bool bearishSupplyRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and supplyZoneActive and not supplyZoneRetested and not supplyZoneInvalidated and not supplyZoneExpired and bar_index > supplyZoneCreatedBar and high >= supplyZoneLow and low <= supplyZoneHigh and close < supplyZoneLow and close < open

if bullishDemandRetest
    demandZoneRetested := true
if bearishSupplyRetest
    supplyZoneRetested := true

if demandZoneInvalidated or demandZoneExpired
    demandZoneActive := false
    demandZoneRetested := false
    if not na(demandZoneBox)
        box.delete(demandZoneBox)
        demandZoneBox := na
    demandZoneHigh := na
    demandZoneLow := na
    demandZoneCreatedBar := na
    demandZoneBaseCount := 0
    demandZonePatternCode := 0
if supplyZoneInvalidated or supplyZoneExpired
    supplyZoneActive := false
    supplyZoneRetested := false
    if not na(supplyZoneBox)
        box.delete(supplyZoneBox)
        supplyZoneBox := na
    supplyZoneHigh := na
    supplyZoneLow := na
    supplyZoneCreatedBar := na
    supplyZoneBaseCount := 0
    supplyZonePatternCode := 0

f_refresh_supply_demand_captions(barstate.islast, demandZoneBox, supplyZoneBox, bar_index + visualPlanBoxOffsetBars, bullColor, bearColor)

int zoneSignal = bullishDemandRetest ? 1 : bearishSupplyRetest ? -1 : demandZoneActive and close > demandZoneHigh and resolvedStructureBias == 1 ? 1 : supplyZoneActive and close < supplyZoneLow and resolvedStructureBias == -1 ? -1 : 0
int zoneStateCode = f_zone_state_code(bullishDemandRetest, bearishSupplyRetest, demandZoneInvalidated, supplyZoneInvalidated, demandZoneExpired, supplyZoneExpired, demandZoneActive, supplyZoneActive)
string demandOriginState = demandZoneActive ? f_zone_pattern_text(demandZonePatternCode, demandZoneBaseCount) : ""
string supplyOriginState = supplyZoneActive ? f_zone_pattern_text(supplyZonePatternCode, supplyZoneBaseCount) : ""
string activeZoneOriginState = demandZoneActive and supplyZoneActive ? demandOriginState + " | " + supplyOriginState : demandZoneActive ? demandOriginState : supplyZoneActive ? supplyOriginState : ""
string zoneState = f_zone_state_text(zoneStateCode) + (activeZoneOriginState == "" ? "" : "\n" + activeZoneOriginState + " | حد قريب من الجسم وحد بعيد من الذيل")

int technicalOpinionCode = f_technical_opinion_code(structureDirectionConflict, channelLayerConflict, convergingImportantStructure, majorChannelUpperFakeout or channelUpperFakeout, majorChannelLowerFakeout or channelLowerFakeout, majorChannelReady or channelReady, effectiveChannelBias, majorChannelBreakoutDown or channelBreakoutDown, majorChannelBreakoutUp or channelBreakoutUp, resolvedStructureBias, effectiveChannelSignal)
string technicalOpinion = f_technical_opinion_text(technicalOpinionCode)

if bullishSweep
    lowWasSwept := true
if bearishSweep
    highWasSwept := true

// Watch a confirmed break for either a successful retest or a multi-bar failure.
var int watchedBreakDirection = 0
var float watchedBreakLevel = na
var int watchedBreakBar = na

if bullishBreak
    watchedBreakDirection := 1
    watchedBreakLevel := lastSwingHigh
    watchedBreakBar := bar_index
else if bearishBreak
    watchedBreakDirection := -1
    watchedBreakLevel := lastSwingLow
    watchedBreakBar := bar_index

int barsFromBreak = na(watchedBreakBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - watchedBreakBar
bool watchAfterBreak = watchedBreakDirection != 0 and barsFromBreak > 0
bool insideFakeoutWindow = watchAfterBreak and barsFromBreak <= fakeoutWindow
bool insideRetestWindow = watchAfterBreak and barsFromBreak <= retestWindow
float retestTolerance = safeAtr * retestAtrTolerance

float failureConfirmationBuffer = breakBuffer * 0.25
bool bullishBreakFailed = barstate.isconfirmed and insideFakeoutWindow and watchedBreakDirection == 1 and close < watchedBreakLevel - failureConfirmationBuffer
bool bearishBreakFailed = barstate.isconfirmed and insideFakeoutWindow and watchedBreakDirection == -1 and close > watchedBreakLevel + failureConfirmationBuffer
bool bullishRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideRetestWindow and watchedBreakDirection == 1 and not bullishBreakFailed and low >= watchedBreakLevel - retestTolerance and low <= watchedBreakLevel + retestTolerance and close > watchedBreakLevel and closeLocation >= 0.55
bool bearishRetest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideRetestWindow and watchedBreakDirection == -1 and not bearishBreakFailed and high <= watchedBreakLevel + retestTolerance and high >= watchedBreakLevel - retestTolerance and close < watchedBreakLevel and closeLocation <= 0.45

// Wyckoff spring/upthrust tests require a later revisit with a smaller result
// and without renewed high effort. The raw sweep alone remains a warning.
var int watchedSweepDirection = 0
var float watchedSweepLevel = na
var float watchedSweepExtreme = na
var int watchedSweepBar = na
if twoSidedLiquiditySweep
    watchedSweepDirection := 0
    watchedSweepLevel := na
    watchedSweepExtreme := na
    watchedSweepBar := na
else if bullishLiquiditySweep
    watchedSweepDirection := 1
    watchedSweepLevel := lastSwingLow
    watchedSweepExtreme := low
    watchedSweepBar := bar_index
else if bearishLiquiditySweep
    watchedSweepDirection := -1
    watchedSweepLevel := lastSwingHigh
    watchedSweepExtreme := high
    watchedSweepBar := bar_index

int barsFromSweep = na(watchedSweepBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - watchedSweepBar
bool insideSweepTestWindow = watchedSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromSweep > 0 and barsFromSweep <= retestWindow
bool quieterTestVolume = volumeAvailable and relativeVolume <= 1.0
bool quieterTestSpread = not na(relativeSpread) and relativeSpread <= 1.0
bool bullishWyckoffTest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideSweepTestWindow and watchedSweepDirection == 1 and low <= watchedSweepLevel + retestTolerance and low > watchedSweepExtreme and close > watchedSweepLevel and close > open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
bool bearishWyckoffTest = barstate.isconfirmed and insideSweepTestWindow and watchedSweepDirection == -1 and high >= watchedSweepLevel - retestTolerance and high < watchedSweepExtreme and close < watchedSweepLevel and close < open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
float wyckoffTestLevel = bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest ? watchedSweepLevel : na
float wyckoffTestExtreme = bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest ? watchedSweepExtreme : na
if bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or barsFromSweep > retestWindow
    watchedSweepDirection := 0
    watchedSweepLevel := na
    watchedSweepExtreme := na
    watchedSweepBar := na

// =============================================================================
// Wyckoff accumulation/distribution sequence
// =============================================================================
// This is a contextual state machine, not a claim that every range is operated
// by one entity. Phase A needs a prior trend plus effort; AR/ST build the range;
// Phase C is optional; Phase D needs a confirmed SOS/SOW and its retest.
var int wyckoffMode = 0              // 1 accumulation, -1 distribution, 0 none
var int wyckoffStage = 0             // event progression inside the active mode
var int wyckoffPhase = 0             // 1=A, 2=B, 3=C, 4=D, 5=E
var int wyckoffSequenceStartBar = na
var int wyckoffClimaxBar = na
var int wyckoffAutomaticBar = na
var int wyckoffSignBar = na
var int wyckoffPhaseEBar = na
var float wyckoffRangeSupport = na
var float wyckoffRangeResistance = na
var float wyckoffClimaxRvol = na
var float wyckoffClimaxSpread = na
var bool wyckoffResistanceWasBroken = false
var bool wyckoffSupportWasBroken = false
// Phase-C tests must remain tied to the exact Spring/UTAD that created them.
// They do not reuse the general liquidity-sweep watcher.
var int wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection = 0
var float wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel = na
var float wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme = na
var int wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar = na
var array<label> wyckoffModelLabels = array.new<label>()
var line wyckoffSupportLine = na
var line wyckoffResistanceLine = na

// Original accumulation must stop an extended downtrend; original distribution
// must stop an extended uptrend. Re-accumulation/re-distribution are intentionally
// not labelled by this state machine because their prior-trend rules are different.
int adaptiveWyckoffTrendMinimum = chartSeconds <= 5 * 60 ? 60 : chartSeconds <= 30 * 60 ? 40 : chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 30 : chartSeconds <= 24 * 60 * 60 ? 40 : chartSeconds <= 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 ? 26 : 12
int wyckoffPriorTrendBars = math.max(wyckoffTrendLookback, adaptiveWyckoffTrendMinimum)
int wyckoffTrendSlopeBars = math.max(3, int(math.round(float(wyckoffPriorTrendBars) * 0.25)))
float wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr = chartSeconds < 24 * 60 * 60 ? 3.0 : 4.0
float priorWyckoffLow = ta.lowest(low[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars)
float priorWyckoffHigh = ta.highest(high[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars)
float wyckoffTrendStartClose = close[wyckoffPriorTrendBars + 1]
float wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr = nz(ta.sma(safeAtr[1], wyckoffPriorTrendBars), safeAtr)
float wyckoffLowerWick = math.min(open, close) - low
float wyckoffUpperWick = high - math.max(open, close)
bool wyckoffTrendHistoryReady = not na(wyckoffTrendStartClose) and not na(ema20[1]) and not na(ema50[1]) and not na(ema20[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]) and not na(ema50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]) and not na(priorWyckoffLow) and not na(priorWyckoffHigh)
// Qualify the trend from bars preceding the stopping event. The climax candle
// may itself reverse sharply, so requiring its close to remain below/above the
// moving averages would contradict the purpose of SC/BC detection.
bool extendedDownwardMove = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and wyckoffTrendStartClose - low >= wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr * wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr
bool extendedUpwardMove = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and high - wyckoffTrendStartClose >= wyckoffTrendReferenceAtr * wyckoffMinimumTrendMoveAtr
bool persistentDowntrend = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and ema20[1] < ema50[1] and close[1] < ema20[1] and ema20[1] < ema20[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1] and ema50[1] <= ema50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]
bool persistentUptrend = wyckoffTrendHistoryReady and ema20[1] > ema50[1] and close[1] > ema20[1] and ema20[1] > ema20[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1] and ema50[1] >= ema50[wyckoffTrendSlopeBars + 1]
bool priorDowntrendForWyckoff = extendedDownwardMove and persistentDowntrend
bool priorUptrendForWyckoff = extendedUpwardMove and persistentUptrend
bool preliminaryWyckoffEffort = volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= wyckoffPreliminaryVolume
bool climaxWyckoffEffort = volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= wyckoffClimaxVolume
bool preliminarySupportRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorDowntrendForWyckoff and preliminaryWyckoffEffort and close < open and (vsaWideResult or wyckoffLowerWick >= candleRange * 0.15) and (na(priorWyckoffLow) or low <= priorWyckoffLow)
bool sellingClimaxRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorDowntrendForWyckoff and climaxWyckoffEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.30 and wyckoffLowerWick >= candleRange * 0.15 and (na(priorWyckoffLow) or low <= priorWyckoffLow)
bool preliminarySupplyRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorUptrendForWyckoff and preliminaryWyckoffEffort and close > open and (vsaWideResult or wyckoffUpperWick >= candleRange * 0.15) and (na(priorWyckoffHigh) or high >= priorWyckoffHigh)
bool buyingClimaxRaw = barstate.isconfirmed and priorUptrendForWyckoff and climaxWyckoffEffort and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.70 and wyckoffUpperWick >= candleRange * 0.15 and (na(priorWyckoffHigh) or high >= priorWyckoffHigh)

bool wyckoffBuildingExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode != 0 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) and bar_index - wyckoffSequenceStartBar > wyckoffMaxSequenceBars
bool wyckoffPhaseEOppositeBreak = wyckoffPhase == 5 and ((wyckoffMode == 1 and (bearishChoch or (not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and close < wyckoffRangeSupport - breakBuffer))) or (wyckoffMode == -1 and (bullishChoch or (not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and close > wyckoffRangeResistance + breakBuffer))))
bool wyckoffPhaseEExpired = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode != 0 and wyckoffPhase == 5 and not na(wyckoffPhaseEBar) and (bar_index - wyckoffPhaseEBar > contextLookback or wyckoffPhaseEOppositeBreak)
bool wyckoffSequenceExpired = wyckoffBuildingExpired or wyckoffPhaseEExpired
if wyckoffSequenceExpired
    f_clear_label_array(wyckoffModelLabels)
    if not na(wyckoffSupportLine)
        line.delete(wyckoffSupportLine)
        wyckoffSupportLine := na
    if not na(wyckoffResistanceLine)
        line.delete(wyckoffResistanceLine)
        wyckoffResistanceLine := na
    wyckoffMode := 0
    wyckoffStage := 0
    wyckoffPhase := 0
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := na
    wyckoffClimaxBar := na
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffSignBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := na
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := na
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

bool preliminarySupportEvent = preliminarySupportRaw and not sellingClimaxRaw and (wyckoffMode == 0 or (wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffPhase == 5))
bool sellingClimaxEvent = sellingClimaxRaw and (wyckoffMode != 1 or wyckoffStage <= 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)
bool preliminarySupplyEvent = preliminarySupplyRaw and not buyingClimaxRaw and not preliminarySupportEvent and not sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode == 0 or (wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffPhase == 5))
bool buyingClimaxEvent = buyingClimaxRaw and not sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != -1 or wyckoffStage <= 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)
bool startNewWyckoffModel = preliminarySupportEvent or preliminarySupplyEvent or (sellingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != 1 or wyckoffPhase == 5)) or (buyingClimaxEvent and (wyckoffMode != -1 or wyckoffPhase == 5))

if startNewWyckoffModel
    f_clear_label_array(wyckoffModelLabels)
    if not na(wyckoffSupportLine)
        line.delete(wyckoffSupportLine)
        wyckoffSupportLine := na
    if not na(wyckoffResistanceLine)
        line.delete(wyckoffResistanceLine)
        wyckoffResistanceLine := na
    // Fully clear the previous model before assigning the new event. Without
    // this reset, a fresh PS/PSY could inherit the old range boundary or climax
    // statistics and display a false completed range.
    wyckoffStage := 0
    wyckoffPhase := 0
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := na
    wyckoffClimaxBar := na
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffSignBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := na
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := na
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

int confirmedPivotBar = bar_index - pivotBars
float wyckoffRangeHeight = not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) ? math.max(wyckoffRangeResistance - wyckoffRangeSupport, safeAtr) : safeAtr
float wyckoffRangeTolerance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, wyckoffRangeHeight * 0.20)
bool pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax = volumeAvailable[pivotBars] and not na(wyckoffClimaxRvol) and relativeVolume[pivotBars] <= math.min(1.10, wyckoffClimaxRvol * 0.80)
bool pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax = not na(relativeSpread[pivotBars]) and not na(wyckoffClimaxSpread) and relativeSpread[pivotBars] <= math.min(1.0, wyckoffClimaxSpread * 0.80)
bool accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 2 and newPivotHigh and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffClimaxBar and pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeSupport >= safeAtr
bool distributionAutomaticReactionEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 2 and newPivotLow and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffClimaxBar and wyckoffRangeResistance - pivotLow >= safeAtr
bool accumulationSecondaryTestEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 3 and wyckoffStage <= 4 and newPivotLow and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffAutomaticBar and math.abs(pivotLow - wyckoffRangeSupport) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax and pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax
bool distributionSecondaryTestEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 3 and wyckoffStage <= 4 and newPivotHigh and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffAutomaticBar and math.abs(pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeResistance) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and pivotVolumeQuieterThanClimax and pivotSpreadQuieterThanClimax
bool accumulationSpringEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and bullishLiquiditySweep and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and low < wyckoffRangeSupport - breakBuffer and close > wyckoffRangeSupport
bool distributionUtadEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and bearishLiquiditySweep and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and high > wyckoffRangeResistance + breakBuffer and close < wyckoffRangeResistance
int barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep = na(wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar
bool insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow = wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep > 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep <= retestWindow
bool accumulationSpringTestEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 5 and insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow and wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection == 1 and low <= wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel + retestTolerance and low > wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme and close > wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel and close > open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
bool distributionUtadTestEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 5 and insideWyckoffPhaseCTestWindow and wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection == -1 and high >= wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel - retestTolerance and high < wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme and close < wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel and close < open and quieterTestVolume and quieterTestSpread
float wyckoffPhaseCTestLevel = accumulationSpringTestEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent ? wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel : na
float wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme = accumulationSpringTestEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent ? wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme : na
bool wyckoffResistanceCrossEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffResistanceWasBroken and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and close > wyckoffRangeResistance + breakBuffer and close[1] <= nz(wyckoffRangeResistance[1], wyckoffRangeResistance) + nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
bool wyckoffSupportCrossEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 4 and wyckoffStage < 6 and not wyckoffSupportWasBroken and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and close < wyckoffRangeSupport - breakBuffer and close[1] >= nz(wyckoffRangeSupport[1], wyckoffRangeSupport) - nz(breakBuffer[1], breakBuffer)
bool wyckoffResistanceBreak = wyckoffResistanceCrossEvent and bullishBreakCandleQuality
bool wyckoffSupportBreak = wyckoffSupportCrossEvent and bearishBreakCandleQuality
bool accumulationSosEvent = wyckoffResistanceBreak and volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple and vsaWideResult and closeLocation >= 0.60
bool distributionSowEvent = wyckoffSupportBreak and volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple and vsaWideResult and closeLocation <= 0.40
bool quietWyckoffBackupPivot = volumeAvailable[pivotBars] and relativeVolume[pivotBars] <= 1.10 and not na(relativeSpread[pivotBars]) and relativeSpread[pivotBars] <= 1.0
bool accumulationLpsEvent = wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage >= 6 and wyckoffStage <= 7 and newPivotLow and not na(wyckoffSignBar) and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffSignBar and quietWyckoffBackupPivot and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and math.abs(pivotLow - wyckoffRangeResistance) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and close[pivotBars] > wyckoffRangeResistance and closeLocation[pivotBars] >= 0.55
bool distributionLpsyEvent = wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage >= 6 and wyckoffStage <= 7 and newPivotHigh and not na(wyckoffSignBar) and confirmedPivotBar > wyckoffSignBar and quietWyckoffBackupPivot and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and math.abs(pivotHigh - wyckoffRangeSupport) <= wyckoffRangeTolerance and close[pivotBars] < wyckoffRangeSupport and closeLocation[pivotBars] <= 0.45
bool accumulationPhaseEEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == 1 and wyckoffStage == 7 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and close > wyckoffRangeResistance + safeAtr and ema20 > ema20[1]
bool distributionPhaseEEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and wyckoffMode == -1 and wyckoffStage == 7 and wyckoffPhase < 5 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and close < wyckoffRangeSupport - safeAtr and ema20 < ema20[1]

if wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection != 0 and barsFromWyckoffPhaseCSweep > retestWindow
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na

if sellingClimaxEvent
    int accumulationStartBar = wyckoffMode == 1 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) ? wyckoffSequenceStartBar : bar_index
    wyckoffMode := 1
    wyckoffStage := 2
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := accumulationStartBar
    wyckoffClimaxBar := bar_index
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := low
    wyckoffRangeResistance := na
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := relativeVolume
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := relativeSpread
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if buyingClimaxEvent
    int distributionStartBar = wyckoffMode == -1 and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar) ? wyckoffSequenceStartBar : bar_index
    wyckoffMode := -1
    wyckoffStage := 2
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := distributionStartBar
    wyckoffClimaxBar := bar_index
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := na
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := na
    wyckoffRangeSupport := na
    wyckoffRangeResistance := high
    wyckoffClimaxRvol := relativeVolume
    wyckoffClimaxSpread := relativeSpread
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if preliminarySupportEvent
    wyckoffMode := 1
    wyckoffStage := 1
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := bar_index
    wyckoffRangeSupport := low
else if preliminarySupplyEvent
    wyckoffMode := -1
    wyckoffStage := 1
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffSequenceStartBar := bar_index
    wyckoffRangeResistance := high
else if accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent
    wyckoffStage := 3
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := confirmedPivotBar
    wyckoffRangeResistance := pivotHigh
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := false
else if distributionAutomaticReactionEvent
    wyckoffStage := 3
    wyckoffPhase := 1
    wyckoffAutomaticBar := confirmedPivotBar
    wyckoffRangeSupport := pivotLow
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := false
else if accumulationSecondaryTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 4
    wyckoffPhase := 2
    wyckoffRangeSupport := math.min(wyckoffRangeSupport, pivotLow)
else if distributionSecondaryTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 4
    wyckoffPhase := 2
    wyckoffRangeResistance := math.max(wyckoffRangeResistance, pivotHigh)
else if accumulationSpringEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 1
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := wyckoffRangeSupport
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := low
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := bar_index
else if accumulationSpringTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if distributionUtadEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := -1
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := wyckoffRangeResistance
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := high
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := bar_index
else if distributionUtadTestEvent
    wyckoffStage := 5
    wyckoffPhase := 3
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if accumulationSosEvent
    wyckoffStage := 6
    wyckoffPhase := 4
    wyckoffSignBar := bar_index
    wyckoffResistanceWasBroken := true
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if distributionSowEvent
    wyckoffStage := 6
    wyckoffPhase := 4
    wyckoffSignBar := bar_index
    wyckoffSupportWasBroken := true
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepDirection := 0
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepLevel := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepExtreme := na
    wyckoffPhaseCSweepBar := na
else if accumulationLpsEvent
    wyckoffStage := 7
    wyckoffPhase := 4
else if distributionLpsyEvent
    wyckoffStage := 7
    wyckoffPhase := 4
else if accumulationPhaseEEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent
    wyckoffStage := 8
    wyckoffPhase := 5
    wyckoffPhaseEBar := bar_index

string wyckoffPhaseState = f_wyckoff_phase_state(wyckoffMode, wyckoffPhase)
string wyckoffTrendContextState = f_wyckoff_trend_context_state(wyckoffMode, priorDowntrendForWyckoff, priorUptrendForWyckoff)
bool wyckoffPhaseERecent = wyckoffPhase == 5 and not na(wyckoffPhaseEBar) and bar_index - wyckoffPhaseEBar <= contextLookback
int wyckoffContextSignal = wyckoffMode == 1 and (wyckoffPhase == 3 or wyckoffPhase == 4 or wyckoffPhaseERecent) ? 1 : wyckoffMode == -1 and (wyckoffPhase == 3 or wyckoffPhase == 4 or wyckoffPhaseERecent) ? -1 : 0

int wyckoffEventCode = f_wyckoff_event_code(preliminarySupportEvent, sellingClimaxEvent, accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent, accumulationSecondaryTestEvent, accumulationSpringEvent, accumulationSpringTestEvent, accumulationSosEvent, accumulationLpsEvent, accumulationPhaseEEvent, preliminarySupplyEvent, buyingClimaxEvent, distributionAutomaticReactionEvent, distributionSecondaryTestEvent, distributionUtadEvent, distributionUtadTestEvent, distributionSowEvent, distributionLpsyEvent, distributionPhaseEEvent)
bool wyckoffEventNow = wyckoffEventCode != 0
string wyckoffEventText = f_wyckoff_event_text(wyckoffEventCode)
float wyckoffEventLevel = f_wyckoff_event_level(wyckoffEventCode, low, high, pivotHigh, pivotLow, wyckoffPhaseCTestLevel)
bool wyckoffPivotTimedEvent = accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionAutomaticReactionEvent or distributionSecondaryTestEvent or distributionLpsyEvent
int wyckoffEventBar = wyckoffPivotTimedEvent ? confirmedPivotBar : bar_index
float wyckoffEventAtr = wyckoffPivotTimedEvent ? nz(safeAtr[pivotBars], safeAtr) : safeAtr
bool wyckoffLabelBelow = preliminarySupportEvent or sellingClimaxEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionAutomaticReactionEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent
bool wyckoffRangeEvent = accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or distributionAutomaticReactionEvent or distributionSecondaryTestEvent
bool wyckoffEarlyWarningEvent = preliminarySupportEvent or sellingClimaxEvent or preliminarySupplyEvent or buyingClimaxEvent
bool wyckoffAccumulationEvent = preliminarySupportEvent or sellingClimaxEvent or accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent
string compactWyckoffMarkerText = f_wyckoff_marker_text(wyckoffEventCode, true)
color wyckoffMarkerColor = wyckoffEarlyWarningEvent ? warningColor : wyckoffRangeEvent ? neutralColor : wyckoffAccumulationEvent ? bullColor : bearColor

if wyckoffEventNow and stockChartAllowed and showWyckoffModelLabels
    int effectiveMaxWyckoffLabels = fastestIntradayDisplay ? math.min(maxWyckoffModelLabels, 5) : compactIntradayDisplay ? math.min(maxWyckoffModelLabels, 7) : math.min(maxWyckoffModelLabels, 12)
    while array.size(wyckoffModelLabels) >= effectiveMaxWyckoffLabels
        label oldestWyckoffLabel = array.shift(wyckoffModelLabels)
        if not na(oldestWyckoffLabel)
            label.delete(oldestWyckoffLabel)
    // الرمز الصغير يحدد شمعة الحدث فقط، بينما الشرح الكامل ثابت في اللوحة.
    int wyckoffMarkerLaneCount = compactIntradayDisplay ? 3 : 2
    int wyckoffMarkerLane = array.size(wyckoffModelLabels) % wyckoffMarkerLaneCount
    float wyckoffMarkerBaseOffset = compactIntradayDisplay ? 0.60 : 0.40
    float wyckoffMarkerLaneStep = compactIntradayDisplay ? 0.22 : 0.18
    float wyckoffMarkerOffset = math.max(wyckoffEventAtr * (wyckoffMarkerBaseOffset + wyckoffMarkerLane * wyckoffMarkerLaneStep), syminfo.mintick * (compactIntradayDisplay ? 9.0 : 5.0))
    float wyckoffMarkerPrice = wyckoffLabelBelow ? wyckoffEventLevel - wyckoffMarkerOffset : wyckoffEventLevel + wyckoffMarkerOffset
    string wyckoffChartText = compactWyckoffMarkerText
    label wyckoffMarker = label.new(wyckoffEventBar, wyckoffMarkerPrice, wyckoffChartText, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = wyckoffLabelBelow ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down, color = color.new(wyckoffMarkerColor, compactIntradayDisplay ? 55 : 48), textcolor = color.white, size = size.tiny)
    array.push(wyckoffModelLabels, wyckoffMarker)

bool wyckoffRangeReady = wyckoffMode != 0 and not na(wyckoffRangeSupport) and not na(wyckoffRangeResistance) and not na(wyckoffSequenceStartBar)
string wyckoffRangeState = wyckoffRangeReady ? "دعم النطاق " + f_price(wyckoffRangeSupport) + " | مقاومة النطاق " + f_price(wyckoffRangeResistance) : "حدود نطاق وايكوف غير مكتملة"
if barstate.isconfirmed
    if stockChartAllowed and showWyckoffRangeLines and wyckoffRangeReady
        int wyckoffRangeLeftBar = wyckoffSequenceStartBar
        if na(wyckoffSupportLine)
            wyckoffSupportLine := line.new(wyckoffRangeLeftBar, wyckoffRangeSupport, bar_index + 1, wyckoffRangeSupport, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(bullColor, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        else
            line.set_xy1(wyckoffSupportLine, wyckoffRangeLeftBar, wyckoffRangeSupport)
            line.set_xy2(wyckoffSupportLine, bar_index + 1, wyckoffRangeSupport)
            line.set_color(wyckoffSupportLine, color.new(bullColor, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency))
        if na(wyckoffResistanceLine)
            wyckoffResistanceLine := line.new(wyckoffRangeLeftBar, wyckoffRangeResistance, bar_index + 1, wyckoffRangeResistance, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(bearColor, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        else
            line.set_xy1(wyckoffResistanceLine, wyckoffRangeLeftBar, wyckoffRangeResistance)
            line.set_xy2(wyckoffResistanceLine, bar_index + 1, wyckoffRangeResistance)
            line.set_color(wyckoffResistanceLine, color.new(bearColor, wyckoffRangeLineTransparency))
    else
        if not na(wyckoffSupportLine)
            line.delete(wyckoffSupportLine)
            wyckoffSupportLine := na
        if not na(wyckoffResistanceLine)
            line.delete(wyckoffResistanceLine)
            wyckoffResistanceLine := na

int barsSinceBullishWyckoff = ta.barssince(bullishLiquiditySweep or bearishBreakFailed or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent)
int barsSinceBearishWyckoff = ta.barssince(bearishLiquiditySweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent)
bool recentBullishWyckoff = (not na(barsSinceBullishWyckoff) and barsSinceBullishWyckoff <= contextLookback) or wyckoffContextSignal == 1
bool recentBearishWyckoff = (not na(barsSinceBearishWyckoff) and barsSinceBearishWyckoff <= contextLookback) or wyckoffContextSignal == -1

// Candlestick reversals count only after a prior trend and a following close
// beyond the pattern candle. This prevents isolated doji/hammer guesses.
float realBody = math.max(math.abs(close - open), syminfo.mintick)
float upperWick = high - math.max(open, close)
float lowerWick = math.min(open, close) - low
bool precedingDownContext = resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or (close[1] < ema20[1] and ema20[1] <= ema50[1])
bool precedingUpContext = resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or (close[1] > ema20[1] and ema20[1] >= ema50[1])
bool bullishHammerRaw = precedingDownContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody
bool bearishShootingStarRaw = precedingUpContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody
bool bullishEngulfingRaw = precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and open <= close[1] and close >= open[1] and bodyRatio >= 0.50
bool bearishEngulfingRaw = precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and open >= close[1] and close <= open[1] and bodyRatio >= 0.50
[insideBarRaw, bullishInsideBarSetup, bearishInsideBarSetup, volumeRisingAfterDecline, volumeRisingAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult, sidewaysRangeHigh, sidewaysRangeLow, sidewaysRangeReady, bullishInsideBarBreakout, bearishInsideBarBreakout, bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline, bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance] = f_price_action_breakouts(usePriceActionBreakouts, useCandleConfirmation, sidewaysRangeBars, sidewaysRangeAtrWidth, breakBuffer, safeAtr, volumePass, volumeAvailable, relativeVolume, closeLocation, precedingDownContext, precedingUpContext, bullishBreakCandleQuality, bearishBreakCandleQuality)
bool bullishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and ((bullishHammerRaw[1] or bullishEngulfingRaw[1]) and close > high[1] or bullishInsideBarBreakout)
bool bearishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and ((bearishShootingStarRaw[1] or bearishEngulfingRaw[1]) and close < low[1] or bearishInsideBarBreakout)
int barsSinceBullishCandle = ta.barssince(bullishCandleConfirmation)
int barsSinceBearishCandle = ta.barssince(bearishCandleConfirmation)
bool recentBullishCandle = not na(barsSinceBullishCandle) and barsSinceBullishCandle <= contextLookback
bool recentBearishCandle = not na(barsSinceBearishCandle) and barsSinceBearishCandle <= contextLookback
var string candleState = "لا يوجد نموذج شموعي مؤكد"
if bullishCandleConfirmation
    candleState := bullishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار صاعد مؤكد بكسر الشمعة الأم" : "انعكاس شموعي صاعد مؤكد"
else if bearishCandleConfirmation
    candleState := bearishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار هابط مؤكد بكسر الشمعة الأم" : "انعكاس شموعي هابط مؤكد"

// وصف آخر شمعة مغلقة. هذه قراءة وصفية للشمعة وليست إشارة دخول مستقلة.
bool dojiCandle = bodyRatio <= 0.10
bool strongBullCandle = close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.70 and closeLocation >= 0.75
bool strongBearCandle = close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.70 and closeLocation <= 0.25
bool lowerRejectionCandle = bodyRatio <= 0.45 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody * 1.25
bool upperRejectionCandle = bodyRatio <= 0.45 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody * 1.25
// A horizontal retest is actionable only at a confirmed role-reversal level.
// The candle is confirmation at the correct location, never a standalone trade.
bool bullishHorizontalRetestSetup = barstate.isconfirmed and resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and (bullishCandleConfirmation or strongBullCandle) and vwapLongPass
bool bearishHorizontalRetestSetup = barstate.isconfirmed and supportClusterActsAsResistance and (bearishCandleConfirmation or strongBearCandle) and vwapShortPass
int currentCandleBias = bullishEngulfingRaw or bullishHammerRaw or (lowerRejectionCandle and close >= open) or strongBullCandle ? 1 : bearishEngulfingRaw or bearishShootingStarRaw or (upperRejectionCandle and close <= open) or strongBearCandle ? -1 : 0
int currentCandleCode = f_candle_type_code(bullishEngulfingRaw, bearishEngulfingRaw, bullishHammerRaw, bearishShootingStarRaw, dojiCandle, lowerRejectionCandle, upperRejectionCandle, strongBullCandle, strongBearCandle, insideBarRaw, close, open)
string currentCandleType = f_candle_type_text(currentCandleCode)
string currentCandleMeaning = bullishInsideBarSetup ? "انكماش بعد هبوط؛ انتظر إغلاقا فوق الشمعة الأم" : bearishInsideBarSetup ? "انكماش بعد صعود؛ انتظر إغلاقا أسفل الشمعة الأم" : insideBarRaw ? "انكماش محايد؛ لا دخول قبل كسر الشمعة الأم" : currentCandleBias == 1 ? "أفضلية للمشترين وتحتاج توافق السياق" : currentCandleBias == -1 ? "أفضلية للبائعين وتحتاج توافق السياق" : dojiCandle ? "تردد؛ انتظر كسر أعلى أو أدنى الشمعة" : "لا تعطي إشارة مستقلة"
string currentCandleVolumeText = volumeAvailable ? f_ratio(relativeVolume) : "غير متاح"
string currentCandleDescription = currentCandleType + "\n" + currentCandleMeaning + " | الحجم " + currentCandleVolumeText
bool highVolumeCandle = volumeAvailable and relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple
bool keyRangeCandleNow = barstate.isconfirmed and (vsaWideResult and (strongBullCandle or strongBearCandle) or highVolumeCandle and (vsaWideResult or vsaNarrowResult))
var float lastKeyCandleHigh = na
var float lastKeyCandleLow = na
var int lastKeyCandleBar = na
if keyRangeCandleNow
    lastKeyCandleHigh := high
    lastKeyCandleLow := low
    lastKeyCandleBar := bar_index
else if not na(lastKeyCandleBar) and bar_index - lastKeyCandleBar > keyCandleMaximumAge
    lastKeyCandleHigh := na
    lastKeyCandleLow := na
    lastKeyCandleBar := na

bool highVolumePeakZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingUpContext and (nearStrongResistance or high >= nz(lastSwingHigh, high) - safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation <= 0.55 or upperWick >= realBody)
bool highVolumeBottomZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingDownContext and (nearStrongSupport or low <= nz(lastSwingLow, low) + safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation >= 0.45 or lowerWick >= realBody)
var float highVolumeResistanceHigh = na
var float highVolumeResistanceLow = na
var int highVolumeResistanceBar = na
var float highVolumeSupportHigh = na
var float highVolumeSupportLow = na
var int highVolumeSupportBar = na
bool highVolumeResistanceExpired = not na(highVolumeResistanceBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeResistanceBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or close > highVolumeResistanceHigh + breakBuffer)
bool highVolumeSupportExpired = not na(highVolumeSupportBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeSupportBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or close < highVolumeSupportLow - breakBuffer)
if highVolumeResistanceExpired
    highVolumeResistanceHigh := na
    highVolumeResistanceLow := na
    highVolumeResistanceBar := na
if highVolumeSupportExpired
    highVolumeSupportHigh := na
    highVolumeSupportLow := na
    highVolumeSupportBar := na
if highVolumePeakZoneEvent
    highVolumeResistanceHigh := high
    highVolumeResistanceLow := low
    highVolumeResistanceBar := bar_index
if highVolumeBottomZoneEvent
    highVolumeSupportHigh := high
    highVolumeSupportLow := low
    highVolumeSupportBar := bar_index
bool highVolumeResistanceActive = not na(highVolumeResistanceHigh) and not na(highVolumeResistanceLow)
bool highVolumeSupportActive = not na(highVolumeSupportHigh) and not na(highVolumeSupportLow)
string priceActionState = f_price_action_state_text(insideBarRaw, bullishInsideBarSetup, bearishInsideBarSetup, bullishInsideBarBreakout, bearishInsideBarBreakout, bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline, bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance, sidewaysRangeReady, sidewaysRangeHigh, sidewaysRangeLow)
string volumeCandleLevelsState = f_volume_candle_levels_text(volumeRisingAfterDecline, volumeRisingAfterAdvanceWithWeakResult, highVolumeResistanceActive, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeSupportActive, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportHigh)
bool bullishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation >= 0.55 and (bullishLiquiditySweep or bearishBreakFailed)
bool bearishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation <= 0.45 and (bearishLiquiditySweep or bullishBreakFailed)
bool bullishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close > high[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or bullishLiquiditySweep[1] or bearishBreakFailed[1])
bool bearishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close < low[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or bearishLiquiditySweep[1] or bullishBreakFailed[1])
int vsaSignal = not useVsaAnalysis ? 0 : (bullishVsaConfirmation or bullishVsaAbsorption or bullishVsaEffortResult) ? 1 : (bearishVsaConfirmation or bearishVsaAbsorption or bearishVsaEffortResult) ? -1 : 0
int vsaStateCode = f_vsa_state_code(useVsaAnalysis, bullishVsaConfirmation, bearishVsaConfirmation, volumeAvailable, bullishVsaAbsorption, bearishVsaAbsorption, bullishVsaEffortResult, bearishVsaEffortResult, vsaHighEffort, vsaNarrowResult)
string vsaState = f_vsa_state_text(vsaStateCode)

// Structural AVWAP starts from a confirmed change, liquidity event, or tested zone.
bool structuralVwapAnchorEvent = f_structural_vwap_anchor(bullishChoch, bearishChoch, bullishSweep, bearishSweep, bullishBreakFailed, bearishBreakFailed, bullishWyckoffTest, bearishWyckoffTest, accumulationSosEvent, distributionSowEvent, accumulationLpsEvent, distributionLpsyEvent, bullishDemandRetest, bearishSupplyRetest, channelUpperFakeout, channelLowerFakeout, strongResistanceBreak, strongSupportBreak) or bullishInsideBarBreakout or bearishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance
float structuralVwapBarVolume = nz(volume, 0.0)
var float structuralVwapPriceVolume = na
var float structuralVwapVolume = na

if not useStructuralVwap
    structuralVwapPriceVolume := na
    structuralVwapVolume := na
else if structuralVwapAnchorEvent
    // A new anchor with no valid volume must invalidate the old structural
    // VWAP. Keeping the previous accumulator would falsely label it as anchored
    // to the newest event.
    structuralVwapPriceVolume := structuralVwapBarVolume > 0 ? hlc3 * structuralVwapBarVolume : na
    structuralVwapVolume := structuralVwapBarVolume > 0 ? structuralVwapBarVolume : na
else if not na(structuralVwapVolume) and structuralVwapVolume > 0 and structuralVwapBarVolume > 0
    structuralVwapPriceVolume += hlc3 * structuralVwapBarVolume
    structuralVwapVolume += structuralVwapBarVolume

bool structuralVwapReady = useStructuralVwap and not na(structuralVwapVolume) and structuralVwapVolume > 0
float structuralVwapValue = structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapPriceVolume / structuralVwapVolume : na
float analysisVwapValue = structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapValue : vwapValue
string analysisVwapLabel = structuralVwapReady ? "هيكلي" : "دوري " + effectiveVwapAnchorTimeframe
if barstate.isconfirmed
    confirmedAnalysisVwapValue := analysisVwapValue
    confirmedStructuralVwapReady := structuralVwapReady
// Preserve the exact broken level before the break watcher is reset below.
float confirmedRetestLevel = bullishRetest or bearishRetest ? watchedBreakLevel : na
int generalEventCode = f_general_event_code(wyckoffEventNow, twoSidedLiquiditySweep, bullishBreakFailed, bearishBreakFailed, bullishWyckoffTest, bearishWyckoffTest, bullishLiquiditySweep, bearishLiquiditySweep, channelLowerFakeout, channelUpperFakeout, bullishDemandRetest, bearishSupplyRetest, doubleBottomConfirmed, doubleTopConfirmed, bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline, bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance, bullishCandleConfirmation, bearishCandleConfirmation, bullishRetest, bearishRetest, bullishChoch, bearishChoch, bullishBos, bearishBos)
float rangeBreakLevel = bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline ? sidewaysRangeHigh : bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance ? sidewaysRangeLow : na
float eventAlertLevel = f_general_event_level(generalEventCode, wyckoffEventLevel, close, lastSwingLow, lastSwingHigh, wyckoffTestLevel, demandZoneHigh, supplyZoneLow, channelFakeoutLevel, watchedBreakLevel, rangeBreakLevel)

if bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishRetest or bearishRetest or barsFromBreak > math.max(fakeoutWindow, retestWindow)
    watchedBreakDirection := 0
    watchedBreakLevel := na
    watchedBreakBar := na

var string lastWyckoffEvent = "لا يوجد"
var string lastClassicEvent = "لا يوجد"
var int lastWyckoffEventBar = na
var int lastClassicEventBar = na

if wyckoffEventNow
    lastWyckoffEvent := wyckoffEventText
    if accumulationSosEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "اختراق مقاومة نطاق مؤكد"
    else if distributionSowEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "كسر دعم نطاق مؤكد"
    else if accumulationLpsEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار مقاومة مخترقة كدعم"
    else if distributionLpsyEvent
        lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار دعم مكسور كمقاومة"
else if bullishWyckoffTest
    lastWyckoffEvent := "اختبار سحب سيولة صاعد بحجم وسبريد أهدأ"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختبار دعم ناجح"
else if bearishWyckoffTest
    lastWyckoffEvent := "اختبار سحب سيولة هابط بحجم وسبريد أهدأ"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختبار مقاومة ناجح"
else if twoSidedLiquiditySweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "سحب سيولة من الجانبين؛ حدث محايد يحتاج كسرا لاحقا"
    lastClassicEvent := "شمعة خارجية سحبت سيولة فوق المقاومة وتحت الدعم"
else if bullishSweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "سحب سيولة أسفل الدعم؛ لا يسمى Spring دون نطاق تجميع"
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر وهمي للدعم"
else if bearishSweep
    lastWyckoffEvent := "سحب سيولة أعلى المقاومة؛ لا يسمى UTAD دون نطاق تصريف"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق وهمي للمقاومة"
else if bearishBreakFailed
    lastWyckoffEvent := "مصيدة بائعين بعد كسر فاشل"
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر هابط فاشل"
else if bullishBreakFailed
    lastWyckoffEvent := "مصيدة مشترين بعد اختراق فاشل"
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق صاعد فاشل"
else if doubleBottomConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "قاعان مؤكّدان بعد اختراق خط العنق"
else if doubleTopConfirmed
    lastClassicEvent := "قمتان مؤكّدتان بعد كسر خط العنق"
else if channelLowerFakeout
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر وهمي أسفل القناة"
else if channelUpperFakeout
    lastClassicEvent := "قفزة وهمية أعلى القناة"
else if bullishDemandRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار منطقة طلب"
else if bearishSupplyRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار منطقة عرض"
else if bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق نطاق عرضي ضيق بعد هبوط"
else if bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر نطاق عرضي ضيق بعد صعود"
else if bullishCandleConfirmation or bearishCandleConfirmation
    lastClassicEvent := candleState
else if bullishRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار صاعدة ناجحة"
else if bearishRetest
    lastClassicEvent := "إعادة اختبار هابطة ناجحة"
else if bullishBreak
    lastClassicEvent := "اختراق مقاومة مؤكد"
else if bearishBreak
    lastClassicEvent := "كسر دعم مؤكد"

bool wyckoffDescriptionUpdatedNow = wyckoffEventNow or bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or bullishSweep or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed
bool classicDescriptionUpdatedNow = accumulationSosEvent or distributionSowEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or (not wyckoffEventNow and generalEventCode != 0)
if wyckoffDescriptionUpdatedNow
    lastWyckoffEventBar := bar_index
if classicDescriptionUpdatedNow
    lastClassicEventBar := bar_index
int barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription = na(lastWyckoffEventBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - lastWyckoffEventBar
int barsSinceLastClassicDescription = na(lastClassicEventBar) ? 100000 : bar_index - lastClassicEventBar
string activeWyckoffEventDescription = barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription <= contextLookback ? lastWyckoffEvent + " | " + f_bar_age(barsSinceLastWyckoffDescription) : "لا يوجد حدث وايكوف حديث داخل النموذج"
string activeClassicEventDescription = barsSinceLastClassicDescription <= contextLookback ? lastClassicEvent + " | " + f_bar_age(barsSinceLastClassicDescription) : "لا يوجد حدث كلاسيكي حديث"

// شروحات الأحداث العامة والشموع تُعرض في اللوحة الثابتة؛ لا توضع نصوص طويلة فوق السعر.

// =============================================================================
// Latest trade plan only
// =============================================================================
bool longSetup = entryMode == ENTRY_BREAK ? bullishBreak or bullishChannelBreakSetup or bullishMajorChannelBreakSetup or bullishHorizontalBreakSetup or accumulationSosEvent or bullishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline : bullishRetest or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup or bullishDemandRetest or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationLpsEvent
bool shortSetup = entryMode == ENTRY_BREAK ? bearishBreak or bearishChannelBreakSetup or bearishMajorChannelBreakSetup or bearishHorizontalBreakSetup or distributionSowEvent or bearishInsideBarBreakout or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance : bearishSupplyRetest or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup or bearishRetest or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionLpsyEvent
// VWAP is a true entry gate when enabled, so it applies to both direct breaks
// and confirmed retests. Volume/FVG remain break-specific because a valid
// Wyckoff test can require quieter participation rather than expansion volume.
bool stockBullSetup = stockChartAllowed and longSetup and vwapLongPass
bool stockBearSetup = stockChartAllowed and shortSetup and vwapShortPass
bool actionableLongSetup = stockBullSetup
bool actionableShortSetup = stockBearSetup

// ICT premium/discount is tied to the ordered, confirmed swing leg. It is a
// location preference for retests only, never an independent breakout trigger.
[dealingRangeEvidenceReady, dealingRangeSignal, dealingRangeState] = f_dealing_range_context(usePremiumDiscountContext, entryMode == ENTRY_RETEST, swingLevelsCompatible, lastSwingHigh, lastSwingLow, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLowBar, close)
[fibRetracementReady, fibBullishLeg, fibBearishLeg, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracementState] = f_fib_retracement_context(useFibRetracement, swingLevelsCompatible, lastSwingHigh, lastSwingLow, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLowBar, close)
float fibStopMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
float fibTargetMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
float nearestFibSupportForStop = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, fibStopMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, na, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibResistanceForStop = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, fibStopMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, na, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibSupportForTarget = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, fibTargetMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, na, na, na, na, na)
float nearestFibResistanceForTarget = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, fibTargetMinimumDistance, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, na, na, na, na, na)

// =============================================================================
// التوافق مقسم إلى مجموعات مستقلة قدر الإمكان، ويستخدم مقاما ديناميكيا حسب
// الأدوات المتاحة والمفعلة بدلا من عد الحدث السعري نفسه عدة مرات.
bool swingStructureReady = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) and swingLevelsCompatible
bool higherStructureReady = not na(higherClose) and not na(higherEma20) and not na(higherEma50)
int structureEvidenceMax = (swingStructureReady ? 1 : 0) + (higherStructureReady ? 1 : 0)
int bullishStructureEvidence = (swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (higherStructureReady and higherTrendBias == 1 ? 1 : 0)
int bearishStructureEvidence = (swingStructureReady and resolvedStructureBias == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (higherStructureReady and higherTrendBias == -1 ? 1 : 0)

// Direct RVOL and VSA are correlated because VSA already contains volume. Keep
// them in one participation group so the same candle cannot earn two votes.
bool directVolumeEvidenceEnabled = useVolumeFilter and entryMode == ENTRY_BREAK and volumeAvailable
bool vsaEvidenceEnabled = useVsaAnalysis and volumeAvailable
int participationEvidenceMax = directVolumeEvidenceEnabled or vsaEvidenceEnabled ? 1 : 0
int bullishParticipationEvidence = (directVolumeEvidenceEnabled and relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple and closeLocation >= 0.55) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == 1) ? 1 : 0
int bearishParticipationEvidence = (directVolumeEvidenceEnabled and relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple and closeLocation <= 0.45) or (vsaEvidenceEnabled and vsaSignal == -1) ? 1 : 0

// If the hard VWAP requirement is enabled, score against the very same entry
// reference. With the hard filter disabled, only an explicitly enabled and ready
// structural VWAP contributes soft evidence; periodic VWAP does not sneak back in.
float evidenceVwapValue = useVwapFilter ? entryVwapValue : useStructuralVwap and structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapValue : na
bool vwapEvidenceReady = not na(evidenceVwapValue)
bool zoneEvidenceReady = demandZoneActive or supplyZoneActive
bool horizontalSrEvidenceReady = horizontalSrSignal != 0
int rawLocationEvidenceMax = (channelEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady ? 1 : 0)
int locationEvidenceMax = math.min(3, rawLocationEvidenceMax)
int bullishLocationRaw = (channelEvidenceReady and effectiveChannelSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close > evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == 1 ? 1 : 0)
int bearishLocationRaw = (channelEvidenceReady and effectiveChannelSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (vwapEvidenceReady and close < evidenceVwapValue ? 1 : 0) + (zoneEvidenceReady and zoneSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (horizontalSrEvidenceReady and horizontalSrSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0) + (dealingRangeEvidenceReady and dealingRangeSignal == -1 ? 1 : 0)
int bullishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, bullishLocationRaw)
int bearishLocationEvidence = math.min(locationEvidenceMax, bearishLocationRaw)

int barsSinceBullishRangeBreak = ta.barssince(bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline)
int barsSinceBearishRangeBreak = ta.barssince(bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance)
bool recentBullishRangeBreak = not na(barsSinceBullishRangeBreak) and barsSinceBullishRangeBreak <= contextLookback
bool recentBearishRangeBreak = not na(barsSinceBearishRangeBreak) and barsSinceBearishRangeBreak <= contextLookback
int triggerEvidenceMax = math.min(2, 1 + (useCandleConfirmation ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence ? 1 : 0))
int bullishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (recentBullishWyckoff ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and (recentBullishCandle or recentBullishRangeBreak) ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBullishDivergence ? 1 : 0))
int bearishTriggerEvidence = math.min(triggerEvidenceMax, (recentBearishWyckoff ? 1 : 0) + (useCandleConfirmation and (recentBearishCandle or recentBearishRangeBreak) ? 1 : 0) + (useRsiDivergence and recentBearishDivergence ? 1 : 0))

int evidenceMaximum = math.max(1, structureEvidenceMax + participationEvidenceMax + locationEvidenceMax + triggerEvidenceMax)
int bullishEvidence = bullishStructureEvidence + bullishParticipationEvidence + bullishLocationEvidence + bullishTriggerEvidence
int bearishEvidence = bearishStructureEvidence + bearishParticipationEvidence + bearishLocationEvidence + bearishTriggerEvidence
float bullishEvidencePercent = 100.0 * float(bullishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum)
float bearishEvidencePercent = 100.0 * float(bearishEvidence) / float(evidenceMaximum)
int combinedTechnicalOpinionCode = f_combined_opinion_code(swingLevelsCompatible, structureDirectionConflict, channelLayerConflict, bullishEvidencePercent, bullishEvidence, bearishEvidencePercent, bearishEvidence, resolvedStructureBias)
string combinedTechnicalOpinion = f_combined_opinion_text(combinedTechnicalOpinionCode, technicalOpinion)
bool bullishCandleIsLatest = recentBullishCandle and (not recentBearishCandle or barsSinceBullishCandle <= barsSinceBearishCandle)
bool bullishDivergenceIsLatest = recentBullishDivergence and (not recentBearishDivergence or barsSinceBullishDivergence <= barsSinceBearishDivergence)
string activeCandleState = bullishCandleIsLatest ? "انعكاس شموعي صاعد " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBullishCandle) : recentBearishCandle ? "انعكاس شموعي هابط " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBearishCandle) : "لا يوجد نموذج شموعي حديث"
string activeDivergenceState = bullishDivergenceIsLatest ? "دايفرجنس RSI صاعد " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBullishDivergence) : recentBearishDivergence ? "دايفرجنس RSI هابط " + f_bar_age(barsSinceBearishDivergence) : "لا يوجد دايفرجنس حديث"
string behaviorState = "آخر شمعة مغلقة: " + currentCandleDescription + "\n" + activeCandleState + " | " + activeDivergenceState + "\n" + priceActionState
string higherContextState = higherTrendState + " | صاعد " + str.tostring(bullishEvidence) + "/" + str.tostring(evidenceMaximum) + " مقابل هابط " + str.tostring(bearishEvidence) + "/" + str.tostring(evidenceMaximum)

// The dashboard always displays the last confirmed close, including while a new candle is forming.
var int closedStructureBias = 0
var bool closedStructureConflict = false
var int closedDowBias = 0
var string closedDowState = "عرضي أو انتقالي"
var string closedWyckoffEvent = "لا يوجد"
var string closedWyckoffPhaseState = "لا يوجد تسلسل وايكوف مكتمل"
var string closedWyckoffTrendContextState = "لا يوجد اتجاه سابق ممتد ومؤهل"
var string closedWyckoffRangeState = "حدود نطاق وايكوف غير مكتملة"
var int closedVsaSignal = 0
var string closedVsaState = "لا توجد إشارة VSA قوية"
var string closedVolumeCandleLevelsState = "لا توجد منطقة شمعة حجم مهمة"
var string closedClassicEvent = "لا يوجد"
var int closedZoneSignal = 0
var string closedZoneState = "لا توجد منطقة مؤكدة"
var int closedHorizontalSrSignal = 0
var string closedHorizontalSrState = "لا يوجد مستوى أفقي متعدد اللمسات"
var string closedBehaviorState = "لا يوجد تأكيد سلوكي"
var int closedHigherTrendBias = 0
var string closedHigherContextState = "الإطار الأعلى غير جاهز"
var int closedMajorChannelSignal = 0
var string closedMajorChannelState = "الرئيسي: غير مكتمل"
var int closedChannelSignal = 0
var string closedChannelState = "غير مكتملة"
var string closedTechnicalOpinion = "محايد"
var bool closedVolumeAvailable = false
var float closedRelativeVolume = na
var bool closedRecentBullFvg = false
var bool closedRecentBearFvg = false
var string closedVwapLabel = "دوري"
var string closedDealingRangeState = "سياق العلاوة/الخصم غير جاهز"
var string closedFibRetracementState = "تصحيح فيبوناتشي غير جاهز"
var string closedMomentumContextState = "سياق الزخم غير جاهز"

if barstate.isconfirmed
    closedStructureBias := resolvedStructureBias
    closedStructureConflict := structureDirectionConflict
    closedDowBias := dowBias
    closedDowState := dowState
    closedWyckoffEvent := wyckoffMode == 0 ? "لا يوجد حدث وايكوف حديث" : activeWyckoffEventDescription
    closedWyckoffPhaseState := wyckoffPhaseState
    closedWyckoffTrendContextState := wyckoffTrendContextState
    closedWyckoffRangeState := wyckoffRangeState
    closedVsaSignal := vsaSignal
    closedVsaState := vsaState
    closedVolumeCandleLevelsState := volumeCandleLevelsState
    closedClassicEvent := activeClassicEventDescription
    closedZoneSignal := zoneSignal
    closedZoneState := zoneState
    closedHorizontalSrSignal := horizontalSrSignal
    closedHorizontalSrState := horizontalSrState
    closedBehaviorState := behaviorState
    closedHigherTrendBias := higherTrendBias
    closedHigherContextState := higherContextState
    closedMajorChannelSignal := majorChannelSignal
    closedMajorChannelState := majorChannelState
    closedChannelSignal := channelSignal
    closedChannelState := channelState
    closedTechnicalOpinion := combinedTechnicalOpinion
    closedVolumeAvailable := volumeAvailable
    closedRelativeVolume := relativeVolume
    closedRecentBullFvg := recentBullFvg
    closedRecentBearFvg := recentBearFvg
    closedVwapLabel := analysisVwapLabel
    closedDealingRangeState := dealingRangeState
    closedFibRetracementState := fibRetracementState
    closedMomentumContextState := momentumContextState

// varip is intentional only for the live plan state: it prevents the same
// intrabar touch from sending repeated alerts on every tick. Entries and all
// analytical setups remain close-confirmed. Historical OHLC cannot reconstruct
// the exact order of a stop and target touched within the same candle.
varip int planDirection = 0
varip bool planActive = false
varip int planStartBar = na
varip float planEntry = na
varip float planStop = na
varip float planTarget1 = na

varip int planScore = 0

varip bool target1Done = false

varip bool target3Done = false
varip bool planTrailingStopActive = false
varip int planTrailingStopStartedBar = na
varip int target1HitBar = na
varip int target2HitBar = na
varip bool planCounterTrend = false
varip string planState = "لا توجد خطة"
varip string planTradeText = "لا توجد صفقة"
