varip int planOutcomeKind = 0

var line entryLine = na
var line stopLine = na
var line target1Line = na
var line target2Line = na
var line target3Line = na
var label planBox = na
var label entryLevelLabel = na
var label stopLevelLabel = na
var label target1LevelLabel = na
var label target2LevelLabel = na
var label target3LevelLabel = na
var label activeEntrySignalLabel = na
// قيّم الخطة القائمة أولا. بهذه الأولوية لا تستطيع إشارة جديدة أن تمسح وقفا
// أو هدفا حدث على الشمعة نفسها. لمس الوقف له الأولوية المحافظة، ثم الإبطال، ثم الأهداف.
bool planInvalidatedEvent = false
bool planReverseExitEvent = false
bool stopHitEvent = false
bool stopGapFillEvent = false
bool stopAndTargetSameBarEvent = false
bool stopAfterTargetSameBarEvent = false
bool stopWithUnorderedTargetEvent = false
bool target1HitEvent = false
bool target2HitEvent = false
bool target3HitEvent = false
bool trailingStopRaisedEvent = false
float stopHitPrice = na
float target1HitPrice = na
float target2HitPrice = na
float target3HitPrice = na

bool planStopTouchedNow = planActive and bar_index > planStartBar and (planDirection == 1 ? low <= planStop : high >= planStop)
bool planInvalidatedByClose = planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and bar_index > planStartBar and not na(planInvalidationLevel) and ((planDirection == 1 and close < planInvalidationLevel) or (planDirection == -1 and close > planInvalidationLevel))
bool planBreakFailedNow = planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and bar_index > planStartBar and ((planDirection == 1 and bullishBreakFailed) or (planDirection == -1 and bearishBreakFailed))

if planActive and bar_index > planStartBar
    bool target1Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget1 : low <= planTarget1
    bool target2Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget2 : low <= planTarget2
    bool target3Touched = planDirection == 1 ? high >= planTarget3 : low <= planTarget3

    if planStopTouchedNow
        bool openedBeyondStop = planDirection == 1 ? open < planStop : open > planStop
        // On realtime bars varip tells us whether T1/T2 was already recorded on
        // an earlier tick. Preserve that known order. Only a still-unrecorded
        // target appearing together with the stop is ambiguous and loses to the
        // stop. Historical OHLC naturally takes this conservative branch.
        bool targetCompletedEarlierThisBar = (target1Done and target1HitBar == bar_index) or (target2Done and target2HitBar == bar_index)
        bool unorderedTargetTouchedNow = (not target1Done and target1Touched) or (not target2Done and target2Touched) or (not target3Done and target3Touched)
        bool targetTouchedOnStopBar = targetCompletedEarlierThisBar or unorderedTargetTouchedNow
        float executableStopPrice = openedBeyondStop ? open : planStop
        stopHitEvent := true
        stopGapFillEvent := openedBeyondStop
        stopAndTargetSameBarEvent := targetTouchedOnStopBar
        stopAfterTargetSameBarEvent := targetCompletedEarlierThisBar
        stopWithUnorderedTargetEvent := unorderedTargetTouchedNow
        stopHitPrice := executableStopPrice
        planActive := false
        string stopKindText = planTrailingStopActive ? "وقف حماية الأرباح المتحرك" : "الوقف"
        string stopBaseState = target2Done ? "تم ضرب " + stopKindText + " بعد تحقق الهدف 2" : target1Done ? "تم ضرب " + stopKindText + " بعد تحقق الهدف 1" : "تم ضرب " + stopKindText
        string stopGapState = openedBeyondStop ? " بفجوة؛ التنفيذ المحافظ عند الافتتاح" : ""
        string stopSequenceState = unorderedTargetTouchedNow ? "؛ ظهر الوقف مع هدف غير مسجل في التحديث نفسه فكانت الأولوية المحافظة للوقف" : targetCompletedEarlierThisBar ? "؛ الهدف سُجل أولا لحظيا ثم لُمس الوقف في الشمعة نفسها" : ""
        planState := stopBaseState + stopGapState + stopSequenceState
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 1
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 12, 2)
        if not target1Done
            f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
        if not target2Done
            f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
        if not target3Done
            f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 82, 1)
        if not na(planBox)
            label.delete(planBox)
            planBox := na
    else if planInvalidatedByClose or planBreakFailedNow
        planInvalidatedEvent := true
        planActive := false
        planState := planInvalidatedByClose ? "ألغيت الخطة: إغلاق تجاوز نقطة الإبطال" : "ألغيت الخطة: الكسر أصبح وهميا"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 2
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 78, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 84, 1)
        if not na(planBox)
            label.delete(planBox)
            planBox := na
    else if target3Touched and not target3Done
        if not target1Done
            target1HitBar := bar_index
        if not target2Done
            target2HitBar := bar_index
        target1Done := true
        target2Done := true
        target3Done := true
        target3HitEvent := true
        target3HitPrice := planTarget3
        planActive := false
        planState := "اكتملت الخطة عند الهدف 3"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeBar := bar_index
        planOutcomeKind := 3
        f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 62, 1)
        f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 78, 1)
        if not na(target1Line)
            line.delete(target1Line)
            target1Line := na
        if not na(target2Line)
            line.delete(target2Line)
            target2Line := na
        if not na(target3Line)
            line.delete(target3Line)
            target3Line := na
        if not na(planBox)
            label.delete(planBox)
            planBox := na
    else if target2Touched and not target2Done
        if not target1Done
            target1HitBar := bar_index
        target2HitBar := bar_index
        target1Done := true
        target2Done := true
        target2HitEvent := true
        target2HitPrice := planTarget2
        planState := "تحقق الهدف 2"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        if not na(target1Line)
            line.delete(target1Line)
            target1Line := na
        if not na(target2Line)
            line.delete(target2Line)
            target2Line := na
    else if target1Touched and not target1Done
        target1Done := true
        target1HitBar := bar_index
        target1HitEvent := true
        target1HitPrice := planTarget1
        planState := "تحقق الهدف 1"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        if not na(target1Line)
            line.delete(target1Line)
            target1Line := na

// The stop used to evaluate this bar was fixed on the preceding execution.
// Only after the confirmed close do we calculate the next bar's protected
// stop. This prevents the current high from raising a stop that is then tested
// against the current low when their true intrabar order is unknown.
if planActive and usePostTargetAtrTrail and target1Done and barstate.isconfirmed and postTargetTrailReady
    float rawTrailCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? postTargetLongRaw : postTargetShortRaw
    float breakEvenProtectedCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.max(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry) : math.min(rawTrailCandidate, planEntry)
    float priceValidCandidate = planDirection == 1 ? math.min(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(breakEvenProtectedCandidate, close + syminfo.mintick)
    bool candidateProtectsEntry = planDirection == 1 ? priceValidCandidate >= planEntry : priceValidCandidate <= planEntry
    bool candidateImprovesStop = planDirection == 1 ? priceValidCandidate > planStop + syminfo.mintick : priceValidCandidate < planStop - syminfo.mintick
    if candidateProtectsEntry and candidateImprovesStop
        planStop := priceValidCandidate
        planTrailingStopActive := true
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na(planTrailingStopStartedBar) ? bar_index : planTrailingStopStartedBar
        trailingStopRaisedEvent := true
        planState := target2Done ? "تحقق الهدف 2 | وقف الأرباح المتحرك مفعّل" : "تحقق الهدف 1 | وقف الأرباح المتحرك مفعّل"
        lastPlanResult := planState
        if not na(stopLine)
            line.set_xy1(stopLine, bar_index, planStop)
            line.set_xy2(stopLine, bar_index + 1, planStop)
            line.set_extend(stopLine, extend.right)
            line.set_color(stopLine, color.new(#F23645, planStopLineTransparency))
            line.set_style(stopLine, line.style_dotted)
            line.set_width(stopLine, 1)

bool longEvidencePass = bullishEvidencePercent >= minimumPlanEvidencePercent and bullishEvidence > bearishEvidence
bool shortEvidencePass = bearishEvidencePercent >= minimumPlanEvidencePercent and bearishEvidence > bullishEvidence
// الاتجاهات ليست أصواتا مكررة فقط؛ هي بوابة سلامة. يمنع التعارض البنيوي
// أو تعارض القناتين الخطة العادية. الاستثناء الوحيد هو انعكاس مؤكد غيّر
// الهيكل وامتلك فارق أدلة واضحا مع كسر/اختبار فني معتبر.
float counterTrendEvidenceMinimum = math.max(65.0, minimumPlanEvidencePercent + 10.0)
bool bullishReversalTrigger = bullishChoch or bullishMajorChannelBreakSetup or bullishChannelBreakSetup or bullishHorizontalBreakSetup or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationLpsEvent or bullishRetest
bool bearishReversalTrigger = bearishChoch or bearishMajorChannelBreakSetup or bearishChannelBreakSetup or bearishHorizontalBreakSetup or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance or distributionSowEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionLpsyEvent or bearishRetest
bool bullishConfirmedReversalPass = resolvedStructureBias == 1 and bullishReversalTrigger and bullishEvidencePercent >= counterTrendEvidenceMinimum and bullishEvidence >= bearishEvidence + 2
bool bearishConfirmedReversalPass = resolvedStructureBias == -1 and bearishReversalTrigger and bearishEvidencePercent >= counterTrendEvidenceMinimum and bearishEvidence >= bullishEvidence + 2
bool analysisIntegrityPass = swingLevelsCompatible and not structureDirectionConflict
bool majorLongOpposed = majorTrendReady and (majorChannelSignal == -1 or (majorChannelSignal == 0 and majorChannelBias == -1))
bool majorShortOpposed = majorTrendReady and (majorChannelSignal == 1 or (majorChannelSignal == 0 and majorChannelBias == 1))
bool minorLongOpposed = trendReady and (channelSignal == -1 or (channelSignal == 0 and channelBias == -1))
bool minorShortOpposed = trendReady and (channelSignal == 1 or (channelSignal == 0 and channelBias == 1))
bool longTrendLayersPass = not channelLayerConflict and ((not majorLongOpposed and not minorLongOpposed) or bullishConfirmedReversalPass)
bool shortTrendLayersPass = not channelLayerConflict and ((not majorShortOpposed and not minorShortOpposed) or bearishConfirmedReversalPass)
bool higherLongAlignmentPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or higherTrendBias != -1 or bullishConfirmedReversalPass
bool higherShortAlignmentPass = not requireHigherTimeframeAlignment or higherTrendBias != 1 or bearishConfirmedReversalPass
int requestedPlanDirection = actionableLongSetup and analysisIntegrityPass and longTrendLayersPass and longEvidencePass and higherLongAlignmentPass and not actionableShortSetup ? 1 : actionableShortSetup and analysisIntegrityPass and shortTrendLayersPass and shortEvidencePass and higherShortAlignmentPass and not actionableLongSetup ? -1 : 0
if planActive and barstate.isconfirmed and requestedPlanDirection != 0 and requestedPlanDirection != planDirection
    planReverseExitEvent := true
    planActive := false
    planState := "أغلقت الخطة بسبب إشارة عكسية مؤكدة"
    lastPlanResult := planState
    lastPlanExitBar := bar_index
    planOutcomeBar := bar_index
    planOutcomeKind := 4
    f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 72, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, 82, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target1Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target2Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, 88, 1)

// جميع نهايات الخطة تمر من هنا: أوقف الخطوط، احذف تفاصيل الخطة، واحتفظ
// بعلامة نتيجة واحدة فقط. تتكرر الكتلة أثناء الشمعة الحية حتى تثبت الرسوم بعد الإغلاق.
bool planEndedThisBar = not planActive and not na(planOutcomeBar) and planOutcomeBar == bar_index
if planEndedThisBar
    bool outcomeIsStop = planOutcomeKind == 1
    bool outcomeIsTarget3 = planOutcomeKind == 3
    f_close_plan_line(entryLine, bar_index, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor, 72, 1)
    f_close_plan_line(stopLine, bar_index, #F23645, outcomeIsStop ? 10 : 82, outcomeIsStop ? 2 : 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target1Line, target1Done and not na(target1HitBar) ? target1HitBar : bar_index, #22C55E, target1Done ? 48 : 88, target1Done ? 2 : 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target2Line, target2Done and not na(target2HitBar) ? target2HitBar : bar_index, #22C55E, target2Done ? 38 : 88, target2Done ? 2 : 1)
    f_close_plan_line(target3Line, bar_index, #22C55E, outcomeIsTarget3 ? 8 : 88, outcomeIsTarget3 ? 2 : 1)
    if not na(planBox)
        label.delete(planBox)
        planBox := na
    if not na(entryLevelLabel)
        label.delete(entryLevelLabel)
        entryLevelLabel := na
    if not na(stopLevelLabel)
        label.delete(stopLevelLabel)
        stopLevelLabel := na
    if not na(target1LevelLabel)
        label.delete(target1LevelLabel)
        target1LevelLabel := na
    if not na(target2LevelLabel)
        label.delete(target2LevelLabel)
        target2LevelLabel := na
    if not na(target3LevelLabel)
        label.delete(target3LevelLabel)
        target3LevelLabel := na
    if not na(activeEntrySignalLabel)
        label.delete(activeEntrySignalLabel)
        activeEntrySignalLabel := na
bool planCooldownComplete = na(lastPlanExitBar) or bar_index - lastPlanExitBar > planReentryCooldownBars
bool createNewPlan = barstate.isconfirmed and requestedPlanDirection != 0 and not planActive and not planEndedThisBar and planCooldownComplete
bool newPlanCreatedEvent = false

if createNewPlan
    bool isLong = requestedPlanDirection == 1
    float fallbackDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 1.5, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float fallbackAnchor = isLong ? close - fallbackDistance : close + fallbackDistance
    float baseContextualStopAnchor = f_contextual_stop_anchor(isLong, bullishDemandRetest, demandZoneActive, demandZoneLow, accumulationLpsEvent, pivotLow, wyckoffRangeResistance, accumulationSpringTestEvent, wyckoffPhaseCTestExtreme, bullishWyckoffTest, wyckoffTestExtreme, bullishRetest, confirmedRetestLevel, low, lastSwingLow, bearishSupplyRetest, supplyZoneActive, supplyZoneHigh, distributionLpsyEvent, pivotHigh, wyckoffRangeSupport, distributionUtadTestEvent, bearishWyckoffTest, bearishRetest, high, lastSwingHigh, fallbackAnchor)
    bool protectedContextStop = isLong ? bullishDemandRetest or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or bullishRetest : bearishSupplyRetest or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or bearishRetest
    bool bullishImpulseBreak = isLong and bullishBreak and strongBullCandle and vsaWideResult
    bool bearishImpulseBreak = not isLong and bearishBreak and strongBearCandle and vsaWideResult
    float priceActionStopAnchor = isLong ? bullishInsideBarBreakout ? low[2] : bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline ? sidewaysRangeHigh : bullishImpulseBreak ? low : na : bearishInsideBarBreakout ? high[2] : bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance ? sidewaysRangeLow : bearishImpulseBreak ? high : na
    float nearestFibStopLevel = isLong ? nearestFibSupportForStop : nearestFibResistanceForStop
    float minorChannelStopLevel = isLong ? (channelReady ? channelLowerNow : channelBias == 1 ? channelBaseNow : na) : (channelReady ? channelUpperNow : channelBias == -1 ? channelBaseNow : na)
    float majorChannelStopLevel = isLong ? (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelLowerNow : majorChannelBias == 1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na) : (majorChannelReady ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelBias == -1 ? majorChannelBaseNow : na)
    float nearestChannelStopLevel = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.10, minorChannelStopLevel, majorChannelStopLevel, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float confluenceStopAnchor = f_nearest_price_level(not isLong, close, safeAtr * 0.35, isLong ? lastSwingLow : lastSwingHigh, isLong ? strongSupportLow : strongResistanceHigh, isLong ? demandZoneLow : supplyZoneHigh, nearestChannelStopLevel, isLong ? highVolumeSupportLow : highVolumeResistanceHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleLow : lastKeyCandleHigh, adaptiveMaBias == (isLong ? 1 : -1) ? adaptiveFastAverage : na, adaptiveMaBias == (isLong ? 1 : -1) ? adaptiveSlowAverage : na, isLong ? wyckoffRangeSupport : wyckoffRangeResistance, nearestFibStopLevel)
    float contextualStopAnchor = not na(priceActionStopAnchor) ? priceActionStopAnchor : protectedContextStop ? baseContextualStopAnchor : nz(confluenceStopAnchor, baseContextualStopAnchor)
    bool confluenceStopUsesFib = not protectedContextStop and not na(nearestFibStopLevel) and not na(confluenceStopAnchor) and math.abs(confluenceStopAnchor - nearestFibStopLevel) <= syminfo.mintick
    string stopReferenceText = not na(priceActionStopAnchor) ? " | الوقف خلف شمعة/نطاق الإشارة" : confluenceStopUsesFib ? " | الوقف خلف تصحيح فيبوناتشي" : not protectedContextStop and not na(confluenceStopAnchor) ? " | الوقف خلف أقرب دعم/مقاومة" : ""
    float validInvalidationAnchor = isLong ? math.min(contextualStopAnchor, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(contextualStopAnchor, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float rawStop = isLong ? validInvalidationAnchor - safeAtr * stopAtrBuffer : validInvalidationAnchor + safeAtr * stopAtrBuffer
    float validStop = isLong ? math.min(rawStop, close - syminfo.mintick) : math.max(rawStop, close + syminfo.mintick)
    float risk = math.max(math.abs(close - validStop), syminfo.mintick)
    float swingRange = not na(lastSwingHigh) and not na(lastSwingLow) ? math.max(math.abs(lastSwingHigh - lastSwingLow), risk) : risk * 2.0
    // الوقف والمخاطرة متكيفان أصلا مع ATR وبنية الفاصل الحالي. نطبق
    // هنا معاملا زمنيا معتدلا على الأهداف فقط حتى تكون أهداف الدقيقة
    // والخمس دقائق أقرب، بينما تتسع تدريجيا حتى الأسبوعي والشهري.
    float targetStepRisk = risk * 0.5 * targetHorizonFactor
    float orderedRr1 = rr1 * targetHorizonFactor
    float orderedRr2 = math.max(rr2 * targetHorizonFactor, orderedRr1 + 0.25 * targetHorizonFactor)
    float orderedRr3 = math.max(rr3 * targetHorizonFactor, orderedRr2 + 0.25 * targetHorizonFactor)
    float effectiveFib1 = fib1 * targetHorizonFactor
    float effectiveFib2 = math.max(fib2 * targetHorizonFactor, effectiveFib1 + 0.05 * targetHorizonFactor)
    float effectiveFib3 = math.max(fib3 * targetHorizonFactor, effectiveFib2 + 0.05 * targetHorizonFactor)
    float candidateTarget1 = na
    float candidateTarget2 = na
    float candidateTarget3 = na
    if targetMode == RR_MODE
        candidateTarget1 := isLong ? close + risk * orderedRr1 : close - risk * orderedRr1
        candidateTarget2 := isLong ? close + risk * orderedRr2 : close - risk * orderedRr2
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? close + risk * orderedRr3 : close - risk * orderedRr3
    else
        float brokenLevel = isLong ? nz(lastSwingHigh, close) : nz(lastSwingLow, close)
        float rawTarget1 = isLong ? brokenLevel + swingRange * effectiveFib1 : brokenLevel - swingRange * effectiveFib1
        float rawTarget2 = isLong ? brokenLevel + swingRange * effectiveFib2 : brokenLevel - swingRange * effectiveFib2
        float rawTarget3 = isLong ? brokenLevel + swingRange * effectiveFib3 : brokenLevel - swingRange * effectiveFib3
        candidateTarget1 := isLong ? math.max(rawTarget1, close + targetStepRisk) : math.min(rawTarget1, close - targetStepRisk)
        candidateTarget2 := isLong ? math.max(rawTarget2, candidateTarget1 + targetStepRisk) : math.min(rawTarget2, candidateTarget1 - targetStepRisk)
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? math.max(rawTarget3, candidateTarget2 + targetStepRisk) : math.min(rawTarget3, candidateTarget2 - targetStepRisk)

    float measuredTarget = isLong ? bullishMeasuredTarget : bearishMeasuredTarget
    if not na(measuredTarget) and (isLong ? measuredTarget > close : measuredTarget < close)
        // الهدف المقاس مفيد، لكن لا نسمح له بإلغاء طبيعة الفاصل عبر إسقاط
        // بعيد جدا. الحد يحافظ على مرونة الهدف الثالث من دون قفزة شاذة.
        float measuredTargetDistanceLimit = math.max(math.abs(candidateTarget3 - close), risk * orderedRr3 * 1.25)
        float boundedMeasuredTarget = isLong ? math.min(measuredTarget, close + measuredTargetDistanceLimit) : math.max(measuredTarget, close - measuredTargetDistanceLimit)
        candidateTarget3 := isLong ? math.max(candidateTarget3, boundedMeasuredTarget) : math.min(candidateTarget3, boundedMeasuredTarget)

    // رتّب أقرب مقاومة/دعم بنيوي صالح داخل سلم الأهداف من دون كسر ترتيبها.
    float minimumStructuralTargetDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.25, syminfo.mintick * 4.0)
    float nearestFibTargetLevel = isLong ? nearestFibResistanceForTarget : nearestFibSupportForTarget
    float minorChannelMeasuredTarget = isLong ? bullishChannelMeasuredTarget : bearishChannelMeasuredTarget
    float majorChannelMeasuredTarget = isLong ? bullishMajorChannelTarget : bearishMajorChannelTarget
    float channelMeasuredTarget = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, minorChannelMeasuredTarget, majorChannelMeasuredTarget, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float auxiliaryTargetCandidate = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, measuredTarget, nearestFibTargetLevel, channelMeasuredTarget, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float minorChannelBoundaryTarget = isLong ? channelUpperNow : channelLowerNow
    float majorChannelBoundaryTarget = isLong ? majorChannelUpperNow : majorChannelLowerNow
    float nearestChannelBoundaryTarget = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, minorChannelBoundaryTarget, majorChannelBoundaryTarget, na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float nearestStructuralTarget = f_nearest_price_level(isLong, close, minimumStructuralTargetDistance, isLong ? lastSwingHigh : lastSwingLow, isLong ? strongResistanceLow : strongSupportHigh, isLong ? supplyZoneLow : demandZoneHigh, nearestChannelBoundaryTarget, isLong ? highVolumeResistanceLow : highVolumeSupportHigh, isLong ? lastKeyCandleHigh : lastKeyCandleLow, adaptiveMaBias == (isLong ? -1 : 1) ? adaptiveFastAverage : na, adaptiveMaBias == (isLong ? -1 : 1) ? adaptiveSlowAverage : na, isLong ? wyckoffRangeResistance : wyckoffRangeSupport, auxiliaryTargetCandidate)
    float targetInsertionSpacing = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 2.0, targetStepRisk * 0.20)
    [insertedTarget1, insertedTarget2, insertedTarget3, structuralTargetUsed] = f_insert_structural_target(isLong, candidateTarget1, candidateTarget2, candidateTarget3, nearestStructuralTarget, targetInsertionSpacing)
    candidateTarget1 := insertedTarget1
    candidateTarget2 := insertedTarget2
    candidateTarget3 := insertedTarget3
    bool structuralTargetUsesFib = structuralTargetUsed and not na(nearestFibTargetLevel) and not na(nearestStructuralTarget) and math.abs(nearestStructuralTarget - nearestFibTargetLevel) <= syminfo.mintick
    bool structuralTargetUsesChannel = structuralTargetUsed and not na(channelMeasuredTarget) and not na(nearestStructuralTarget) and math.abs(nearestStructuralTarget - channelMeasuredTarget) <= syminfo.mintick

    float planRiskPercent = close > 0 ? risk / close * 100.0 : 1000.0
    bool riskDistanceValid = planRiskPercent <= maxPlanRiskPercent
    bool stopPriceValid = validStop >= syminfo.mintick and (isLong ? validStop < close : validStop > close)
    bool planNumbersReady = not na(close) and not na(validStop) and not na(risk) and not na(candidateTarget1) and not na(candidateTarget2) and not na(candidateTarget3)
    bool targetPricesPositive = planNumbersReady and candidateTarget1 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget2 >= syminfo.mintick and candidateTarget3 >= syminfo.mintick
    bool targetOrderValid = planNumbersReady and (isLong ? candidateTarget1 > close and candidateTarget2 > candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 > candidateTarget2 : candidateTarget1 < close and candidateTarget2 < candidateTarget1 and candidateTarget3 < candidateTarget2)
    bool planGeometryValid = planNumbersReady and riskDistanceValid and stopPriceValid and targetPricesPositive and targetOrderValid

    if planGeometryValid
        int score = isLong ? bullishEvidence : bearishEvidence
        planDirection := isLong ? 1 : -1
        planActive := true
        planStartBar := bar_index
        planEntry := close
        planStop := validStop
        planTarget1 := candidateTarget1
        planTarget2 := candidateTarget2
        planTarget3 := candidateTarget3
        planInvalidationLevel := validInvalidationAnchor
        planScore := score
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planCounterTrend := higherTrendBias != 0 and (isLong ? higherTrendBias == -1 : higherTrendBias == 1)
        planOutcomeBar := na
        planOutcomeKind := 0
        newPlanCreatedEvent := true
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        planTrailingStopActive := false
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        planTradeText := isLong ? "فرصة صعود السهم" : "فرصة هبوط السهم"
        planPriceContext := priceContextText
        bool planFromMajorChannel = (isLong and bullishMajorChannelBreakSetup) or (not isLong and bearishMajorChannelBreakSetup)
        bool planFromMinorChannel = (isLong and bullishChannelBreakSetup) or (not isLong and bearishChannelBreakSetup)
        string channelSetupSuffix = planFromMajorChannel ? " | اختراق القناة الرئيسية مؤكد بالإغلاق" : planFromMinorChannel ? " | اختراق القناة الفرعية مؤكد بالإغلاق" : ""
        string horizontalSetupSuffix = isLong and bullishHorizontalBreakSetup ? " | اختراق مقاومة أفقية متعددة اللمسات" : not isLong and bearishHorizontalBreakSetup ? " | كسر دعم أفقي متعدد اللمسات" : isLong and bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? " | اختبار مقاومة مخترقة كدعم" : not isLong and bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? " | اختبار دعم مكسور كمقاومة" : ""
        string structuralTargetSuffix = structuralTargetUsesFib ? " | هدف عند تصحيح فيبوناتشي" : structuralTargetUsesChannel ? " | هدف مقاس بعرض القناة" : structuralTargetUsed ? " | هدف بنيوي قريب" : ""
        planSetupSource := f_plan_setup_source(isLong, bullishInsideBarBreakout, bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline, accumulationLpsEvent, bullishDemandRetest, accumulationSpringTestEvent, bullishWyckoffTest, bullishRetest, doubleBottomConfirmed, accumulationSosEvent, bullishChoch, bearishInsideBarBreakout, bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance, distributionLpsyEvent, bearishSupplyRetest, distributionUtadTestEvent, bearishWyckoffTest, bearishRetest, doubleTopConfirmed, distributionSowEvent, bearishChoch) + channelSetupSuffix + horizontalSetupSuffix + stopReferenceText + structuralTargetSuffix
        planTargetHorizon := currentTargetHorizonText
        planState := isLong ? "خطة صعود السهم نشطة" : "خطة هبوط السهم نشطة"

        // هذه هي شمعة القرار الفعلية: لا تظهر العلامة إلا بعد إغلاق الشمعة
        // واكتمال الإعداد والأدلة وإدارة المخاطرة، لا لمجرد اقتراب السعر.
        activeEntrySignalLabel := f_replace_confirmed_entry_marker(activeEntrySignalLabel, isLong, low, high, safeAtr, syminfo.mintick, compactIntradayDisplay)

        if not na(entryLine)
            line.delete(entryLine)
        if not na(stopLine)
            line.delete(stopLine)
        if not na(target1Line)
            line.delete(target1Line)
        if not na(target2Line)
            line.delete(target2Line)
        if not na(target3Line)
            line.delete(target3Line)
        if not na(planBox)
            label.delete(planBox)
            planBox := na
        if not na(entryLevelLabel)
            label.delete(entryLevelLabel)
            entryLevelLabel := na
        if not na(stopLevelLabel)
            label.delete(stopLevelLabel)
            stopLevelLabel := na
        if not na(target1LevelLabel)
            label.delete(target1LevelLabel)
            target1LevelLabel := na
        if not na(target2LevelLabel)
            label.delete(target2LevelLabel)
            target2LevelLabel := na
        if not na(target3LevelLabel)
            label.delete(target3LevelLabel)
            target3LevelLabel := na
        color directionColor = isLong ? bullColor : bearColor
        // One fixed visual language for both rising and falling plans:
        // cyan entry, red stop, and dotted green targets.
        color confirmedEntryColor = isLong ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor
        entryLine := line.new(bar_index, planEntry, bar_index + 1, planEntry, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(confirmedEntryColor, planEntryLineTransparency), style = line.style_solid, width = 1)
        stopLine := line.new(bar_index, planStop, bar_index + 1, planStop, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(#F23645, planStopLineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        target1Line := line.new(bar_index, planTarget1, bar_index + 1, planTarget1, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(#22C55E, planTarget1LineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        target2Line := line.new(bar_index, planTarget2, bar_index + 1, planTarget2, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(#22C55E, planTarget2LineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        target3Line := line.new(bar_index, planTarget3, bar_index + 1, planTarget3, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = color.new(#22C55E, planTarget3LineTransparency), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        int effectivePlanBoxOffset = visualExplanationOffsetBars
        float planSummaryPrice = compactIntradayDisplay ? (isLong ? planTarget2 + safeAtr * 0.70 : planTarget2 - safeAtr * 0.70) : (planTarget1 + planTarget2) * 0.5
        entryLevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planEntry, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = confirmedEntryColor, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        stopLevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planStop, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = #F23645, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        target1LevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planTarget1, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = #22C55E, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        target2LevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planTarget2, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = #22C55E, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        target3LevelLabel := showPlanLevelLabels ? label.new(bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars, planTarget3, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_none, color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = #22C55E, size = size.tiny, textalign = text.align_left) : na
        planBox := showPlanBox ? label.new(bar_index + effectivePlanBoxOffset, planSummaryPrice, "", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(directionColor, compactIntradayDisplay ? 55 : 38), textcolor = color.white, size = size.tiny) : na
    else
        planDirection := 0
        planActive := false
        planStartBar := na
        planEntry := na
        planStop := na
        planTarget1 := na
        planTarget2 := na
        planTarget3 := na
        planInvalidationLevel := na
        planScore := 0
        planScoreMax := evidenceMaximum
        planCounterTrend := false
        target1Done := false
        target2Done := false
        target3Done := false
        planTrailingStopActive := false
        planTrailingStopStartedBar := na
        target1HitBar := na
        target2HitBar := na
        planTradeText := "لا توجد صفقة"
        planPriceContext := priceContextText
        planSetupSource := "الإعداد مرفوض بإدارة المخاطرة"
        planTargetHorizon := currentTargetHorizonText
        planState := not riskDistanceValid ? "لم تنشأ الخطة: الوقف أبعد من " + str.tostring(maxPlanRiskPercent, "#") + "%" : not stopPriceValid ? "لم تنشأ الخطة: سعر الوقف غير صالح" : "لم تنشأ الخطة: الأهداف غير صالحة أو غير مرتبة"
        lastPlanResult := planState

// عزل تحديث الرسومات في دالة يقلل حجم النطاق الرئيسي. الدالة لا تعيد
// إسناد مراجع عامة؛ هي تعدل خصائص line/label فقط، وهي أنواع مرجعية.
f_refresh_plan_display() =>
    if not na(planBox)
        string planContextWarning = planCounterTrend ? "\nتحذير: الخطة عكس اتجاه الإطار " + higherTimeframe : ""
        string planTrailText = planTrailingStopActive ? " | وقف ATR متحرك " + f_price(planStop) : ""
        string detailedPlanText = planTradeText + " — " + planState + planTrailText + "\nالسبب: " + planSetupSource + " | " + planTargetHorizon + " | التوافق " + str.tostring(planScore) + "/" + str.tostring(planScoreMax) + planContextWarning
        string compactPlanText = planTradeText + " | " + planState + "\n" + planSetupSource + " | توافق " + str.tostring(planScore) + "/" + str.tostring(planScoreMax) + planContextWarning
        string planText = compactIntradayDisplay ? compactPlanText : detailedPlanText
        float livePlanSummaryPrice = compactIntradayDisplay ? (planDirection == 1 ? planTarget2 + safeAtr * 0.70 : planTarget2 - safeAtr * 0.70) : (planTarget1 + planTarget2) * 0.5
        label.set_text(planBox, planText)
        label.set_y(planBox, livePlanSummaryPrice)
        label.set_x(planBox, planActive and barstate.islast ? bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars : bar_index)
    if not na(entryLevelLabel)
        int liveLevelLabelX = bar_index + visualExplanationOffsetBars
        float liveLevelTextOffset = math.max(syminfo.mintick * 3.0, safeAtr * (compactIntradayDisplay ? 0.06 : 0.04))
        string entryCaption = planDirection == 1 ? "دخول شراء " : "دخول بيع "
        string stopCaption = planTrailingStopActive ? "وقف حماية الأرباح " : "وقف الخسارة "
        string target1Caption = target1Done ? "تم الهدف 1 ✓ " : "هدف 1 (جني أول) "
        string target2Caption = target2Done ? "تم الهدف 2 ✓ " : "هدف 2 (جني ثان) "
        string target3Caption = target3Done ? "تم الهدف 3 ✓ " : "هدف 3 (نهائي) "
        label.set_xy(entryLevelLabel, liveLevelLabelX, planEntry + liveLevelTextOffset)
        label.set_text(entryLevelLabel, entryCaption + f_price(planEntry))
        label.set_color(entryLevelLabel, color.new(color.black, 100))
        label.set_textcolor(entryLevelLabel, planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor)
        label.set_xy(stopLevelLabel, liveLevelLabelX, planStop + liveLevelTextOffset)
        label.set_text(stopLevelLabel, stopCaption + f_price(planStop))
        label.set_color(stopLevelLabel, color.new(color.black, 100))
        label.set_textcolor(stopLevelLabel, #F23645)
        label.set_xy(target1LevelLabel, liveLevelLabelX, planTarget1 + liveLevelTextOffset)
        label.set_text(target1LevelLabel, target1Caption + f_price(planTarget1))
        label.set_color(target1LevelLabel, color.new(color.black, 100))
        label.set_textcolor(target1LevelLabel, #22C55E)
        label.set_xy(target2LevelLabel, liveLevelLabelX, planTarget2 + liveLevelTextOffset)
        label.set_text(target2LevelLabel, target2Caption + f_price(planTarget2))
        label.set_color(target2LevelLabel, color.new(color.black, 100))
        label.set_textcolor(target2LevelLabel, #22C55E)
        label.set_xy(target3LevelLabel, liveLevelLabelX, planTarget3 + liveLevelTextOffset)
        label.set_text(target3LevelLabel, target3Caption + f_price(planTarget3))
        label.set_color(target3LevelLabel, color.new(color.black, 100))
        label.set_textcolor(target3LevelLabel, #22C55E)
    true

f_refresh_plan_display()

// Arabic display and theory dashboard

color fibRetracementVisualColor = fibBullishLeg ? bullColor : fibBearishLeg ? bearColor : neutralColor
// Fibonacci remains fully active in analysis, confluence, stop selection and
// target geometry, but its raw levels are intentionally hidden from the chart.
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement236 : na, "تصحيح فيبوناتشي 23.6%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 88), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars, display = display.none)
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement382 : na, "تصحيح فيبوناتشي 38.2%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 80), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars, display = display.none)
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement500 : na, "تصحيح فيبوناتشي 50%", color = color.new(neutralColor, 72), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars, display = display.none)
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement618 : na, "تصحيح فيبوناتشي 61.8%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 78), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars, display = display.none)
plot(stockChartAllowed and showFibRetracement and fibRetracementReady ? fibRetracement786 : na, "تصحيح فيبوناتشي 78.6%", color = color.new(fibRetracementVisualColor, 86), linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr, show_last = fibRetracementDisplayBars, display = display.none)
float channelMeasuredObjectivePlot = not na(bullishChannelMeasuredTarget) ? bullishChannelMeasuredTarget : bearishChannelMeasuredTarget
color channelMeasuredObjectiveColor = color.new(#22C55E, 72)
float majorChannelObjectivePlot = not na(bullishMajorChannelTarget) ? bullishMajorChannelTarget : bearishMajorChannelTarget
color majorChannelObjectiveColor = color.new(#22C55E, 58)
// أهداف القنوات تبقى مدخلات للتحليل والخطة، لكن رسمها الخام كان يظهر

plot(stockChartAllowed and showVwap ? confirmedAnalysisVwapValue : na, "VWAP المستخدم في التحليل", color = color.new(#2962FF, 10), linewidth = 2)
// شمعة الدخول المؤكدة لها الأولوية البصرية حتى لو كان تلوين الاتجاه العام
// معطلا. بقية الشموع لا تتغير إلا إذا فعّل المستخدم تلوين الاتجاه.
barcolor(stockChartAllowed ? newPlanCreatedEvent ? (planDirection == 1 ? confirmedLongEntryColor : confirmedShortEntryColor) : colorBars ? (closedStructureBias == 1 ? color.new(bullColor, 45) : closedStructureBias == -1 ? color.new(bearColor, 45) : na) : na : na)

var table dashboard = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 12, bgcolor = color.new(#111827, 12), frame_color = color.new(neutralColor, 45), frame_width = 1, border_color = color.new(neutralColor, 78), border_width = 1)
int dashboardLabelColumnWidth = 8
int dashboardValueColumnWidth = 36
int dashboardRegularTextSize = 8
int dashboardCompactTextSize = 6
if barstate.isfirst
    table.merge_cells(dashboard, 0, 0, 1, 0)

// table.* can safely mutate the passed reference inside a function. Keeping this
// renderer outside the global body avoids CE10295 without changing calculations.
f_render_dashboard(table dashboardId) =>
    if showDashboard
        string trendText = closedStructureConflict ? "متعارض" : closedStructureBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : closedStructureBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "محايد"
        bool dowAndBreakDiverge = closedStructureBias != 0 and closedDowBias != 0 and closedStructureBias != closedDowBias
        color trendColor = closedStructureConflict or dowAndBreakDiverge ? warningColor : closedStructureBias == 1 ? bullColor : closedStructureBias == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
        bool majorMinorChannelConflict = closedMajorChannelSignal != 0 and closedChannelSignal != 0 and closedMajorChannelSignal != closedChannelSignal
        int closedEffectiveChannelSignal = majorMinorChannelConflict ? 0 : closedMajorChannelSignal != 0 ? closedMajorChannelSignal : closedChannelSignal
        color channelTextColor = closedEffectiveChannelSignal == 1 ? bullColor : closedEffectiveChannelSignal == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
        color vsaTextColor = closedVsaSignal == 1 ? bullColor : closedVsaSignal == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
        color zoneTextColor = closedZoneSignal == 1 ? bullColor : closedZoneSignal == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
        color horizontalSrTextColor = closedHorizontalSrSignal == 1 ? bullColor : closedHorizontalSrSignal == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
        bool zoneSrSignalConflict = closedZoneSignal != 0 and closedHorizontalSrSignal != 0 and closedZoneSignal != closedHorizontalSrSignal
        bool majorHigherSignalConflict = closedMajorChannelSignal != 0 and closedHigherTrendBias != 0 and closedMajorChannelSignal != closedHigherTrendBias
        bool minorHigherSignalConflict = closedChannelSignal != 0 and closedHigherTrendBias != 0 and closedChannelSignal != closedHigherTrendBias
        bool channelHigherSignalConflict = majorHigherSignalConflict or minorHigherSignalConflict
        color zoneSrCellTextColor = zoneSrSignalConflict ? warningColor : closedHorizontalSrSignal != 0 ? horizontalSrTextColor : zoneTextColor
        color channelCellTextColor = majorMinorChannelConflict or channelHigherSignalConflict ? warningColor : closedMajorChannelSignal != 0 or closedChannelSignal != 0 ? channelTextColor : closedHigherTrendBias == 1 ? bullColor : closedHigherTrendBias == -1 ? bearColor : neutralColor
        bool vsaOpinionConflict = closedStructureBias != 0 and closedVsaSignal != 0 and closedStructureBias != closedVsaSignal
        bool higherOpinionConflict = closedStructureBias != 0 and closedHigherTrendBias != 0 and closedStructureBias != closedHigherTrendBias
        bool zoneOpinionConflict = closedStructureBias != 0 and closedZoneSignal != 0 and closedStructureBias != closedZoneSignal
        bool horizontalSrOpinionConflict = closedStructureBias != 0 and closedHorizontalSrSignal != 0 and closedStructureBias != closedHorizontalSrSignal
        color opinionTextColor = closedStructureConflict or majorMinorChannelConflict or channelHigherSignalConflict or vsaOpinionConflict or higherOpinionConflict or zoneOpinionConflict or horizontalSrOpinionConflict ? warningColor : trendColor
        string volumeText = closedVolumeAvailable ? f_ratio(closedRelativeVolume) : "غير متاح"
        string fvgDirectionText = closedRecentBullFvg and closedRecentBearFvg ? "صاعدة وهابطة" : closedRecentBullFvg ? "صاعدة" : closedRecentBearFvg ? "هابطة" : "غير موجودة"
        string fvgText = closedRecentBullFvg or closedRecentBearFvg ? fvgDirectionText + (confirmFvgDisplacement ? " مؤكدة" : "") : fvgDirectionText
        string volumeStatusText = not closedVolumeAvailable ? "غير متاح" : closedRelativeVolume >= volumeMultiple ? "مؤكد" : "ضعيف"
        string volumeFilterText = useVolumeFilter ? entryMode == ENTRY_BREAK ? "حجم الكسر " + volumeStatusText : "حجم الكسر مفعّل ولا يفرض على الاختبار" : "فلتر حجم الكسر معطل"
        string vwapFilterText = useVwapFilter ? "اشتراط VWAP " + entryVwapLabel : "اشتراط VWAP معطل"
        string structuralVwapText = useStructuralVwap ? "السياق " + closedVwapLabel : "VWAP الهيكلي معطل"
        string filtersText = volumeFilterText + " | " + vwapFilterText + " | " + structuralVwapText + " | FVG للكسر " + fvgText
        string chartDisplayText = compactIntradayDisplay ? "عرض لحظي مختصر ومبعد عن الشموع" : "عرض تفصيلي للفاصل"
        string compactModeText = "تحليل فاصل " + timeframe.period + " | دخول بعد الإغلاق\nوقف وأهداف عند اللمس | " + currentTargetHorizonText + " | " + chartDisplayText + "\nمحاور البنية " + str.tostring(pivotBars) + "+" + str.tostring(pivotBars) + " | القناة " + str.tostring(channelPivotBars) + "+" + str.tostring(channelPivotBars) + " | الإطار الأعلى " + higherTimeframe + "\nفيبوناتشي مستخدم داخليا ومخفي بصريا | لا أهداف بلا فرصة نشطة"
        bool activePlanFrameConflict = planActive and planCounterTrend
        string currentFrameBiasText = closedStructureBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : closedStructureBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "انتقالي"
        string higherFrameBiasText = closedHigherTrendBias == 1 ? "صاعد" : closedHigherTrendBias == -1 ? "هابط" : "انتقالي"
        string fixedContextWarning = not showContextWarningLabel ? "" : activePlanFrameConflict ? "\nتحذير: الخطة عكس اتجاه الإطار " + higherTimeframe : higherOpinionConflict ? "\nاختلاف الفواصل: " + timeframe.period + " " + currentFrameBiasText + " | " + higherTimeframe + " " + higherFrameBiasText : ""
        string directionAndDowText = "انحياز الكسر المؤكد: " + trendText + "\nداو: " + closedDowState + (dowAndBreakDiverge ? "\nتنبيه: الكسر وتسلسل المحاور غير متوافقين" : "") + fixedContextWarning
        string dashboardWyckoffEventText = showWyckoffModelLabels ? closedWyckoffEvent : "شرح حدث وايكوف مخفي"
        string dashboardCandleText = showImportantCandleLabel ? closedBehaviorState : "شرح الشمعة مخفي"
        string dashboardClassicEventText = showEventLabel ? closedClassicEvent : "شرح الحدث العام مخفي"
        string dashboardPlanSide = planDirection == 1 ? "صعود السهم" : "هبوط السهم"
        string planCounterTrendText = planActive and planCounterTrend ? "\nتحذير: عكس اتجاه الإطار " + higherTimeframe : ""
        string planCurrentBiasText = planActive and closedStructureBias != 0 and closedStructureBias != planDirection ? "\nتنبيه: الخطة النشطة تخالف انحياز الكسر الحالي" : ""
        string dashboardTrailText = planActive and planTrailingStopActive ? " | وقف متحرك " + f_price(planStop) : ""
        string activePlanText = dashboardPlanSide + " | " + planSetupSource + " | " + planState + dashboardTrailText + " | " + planTargetHorizon + " | " + str.tostring(planScore) + "/" + str.tostring(planScoreMax) + planCounterTrendText + planCurrentBiasText
        string waitingSetupText = actionableLongSetup and actionableShortSetup ? "تعارض إعداد صاعد وهابط؛ لا تُنشأ خطة" : (actionableLongSetup or actionableShortSetup) and not analysisIntegrityPass ? "الإعداد مرفوض: القمم والقيعان أو بنية داو/الكسر متعارضة" : actionableLongSetup and not longTrendLayersPass ? "الإعداد الصاعد مرفوض: الاتجاه الرئيسي أو الفرعي معارض ولم يكتمل انعكاس قوي" : actionableShortSetup and not shortTrendLayersPass ? "الإعداد الهابط مرفوض: الاتجاه الرئيسي أو الفرعي معارض ولم يكتمل انعكاس قوي" : actionableLongSetup and not longEvidencePass ? "إعداد صاعد تحت المراقبة | التوافق " + str.tostring(bullishEvidencePercent, "#.0") + "% من المطلوب " + str.tostring(minimumPlanEvidencePercent, "#") + "%" : actionableShortSetup and not shortEvidencePass ? "إعداد هابط تحت المراقبة | التوافق " + str.tostring(bearishEvidencePercent, "#.0") + "% من المطلوب " + str.tostring(minimumPlanEvidencePercent, "#") + "%" : actionableLongSetup and not higherLongAlignmentPass ? "إعداد صاعد مرفوض مؤقتا بسبب اتجاه الإطار الأعلى" : actionableShortSetup and not higherShortAlignmentPass ? "إعداد هابط مرفوض مؤقتا بسبب اتجاه الإطار الأعلى" : entryMode == ENTRY_RETEST ? "بانتظار إعادة اختبار مؤكدة مكتملة الشروط" : "بانتظار كسر أو تغير سلوك مؤكد مكتمل الشروط"
        string planText = planActive ? activePlanText : "لا توجد فرصة دخول مكتملة الآن\nالمراقبة: " + waitingSetupText + "\nآخر نتيجة: " + lastPlanResult
        string entryStopText = planActive ? (planDirection == 1 ? "فرصة صعود | " : "فرصة هبوط | ") + "دخول " + f_price(planEntry) + " | وقف " + f_price(planStop) : "لا دخول ولا وقف قبل اكتمال الفرصة"
        string targetsText = planActive ? "1: " + f_price(planTarget1) + (target1Done ? " ✓" : "") + " | 2: " + f_price(planTarget2) + (target2Done ? " ✓" : "") + " | 3: " + f_price(planTarget3) : "لا أهداف بلا خطة نشطة"
        color planTextColor = planActive ? planDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor : color.white
        color labelBackground = color.new(neutralColor, 68)
        color valueBackground = color.new(#111827, 12)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 0, "ملخص التحليل المؤكد — SC-V42", text_color = color.white, bgcolor = color.new(neutralColor, 35), text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_center)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 1, "الحالة", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 1, compactModeText, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardCompactTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 2, "الاتجاه / داو", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 2, directionAndDowText, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = trendColor, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 3, "وايكوف", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 3, closedWyckoffPhaseState + "\n" + closedWyckoffTrendContextState + "\n" + closedWyckoffRangeState + "\nآخر حدث: " + dashboardWyckoffEventText, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardCompactTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 4, "الحجم / VSA", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 4, "الحجم " + volumeStatusText + " (" + volumeText + ")\n" + closedVsaState + "\n" + closedVolumeCandleLevelsState, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = vsaTextColor, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardCompactTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 5, "آخر شمعة مغلقة", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 5, dashboardCandleText, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 6, "الكلاسيكي / شرح المناطق", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 6, dashboardClassicEventText + "\n" + closedZoneState + "\n" + closedDealingRangeState + "\n" + closedFibRetracementState + "\n" + closedHorizontalSrState, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = zoneSrCellTextColor, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardCompactTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 7, "الترند / القناة", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        string majorMinorRelationText = majorMinorChannelConflict ? "تنبيه: الاتجاه الرئيسي والفرعي متعارضان" : closedMajorChannelSignal != 0 and closedChannelSignal != 0 ? "الرئيسي والفرعي متوافقان" : closedMajorChannelSignal != 0 ? "الرئيسي اتجاهي | الفرعي غير مكتمل أو محايد" : closedChannelSignal != 0 ? "الفرعي اتجاهي | الرئيسي غير مكتمل أو محايد" : "بانتظار اكتمال اتجاه إحدى الطبقتين"
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 7, closedMajorChannelState + "\nالفرعي: " + closedChannelState + "\n" + majorMinorRelationText + "\n" + channelPivotRuleText + "\n" + closedHigherContextState, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = channelCellTextColor, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardCompactTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 8, "الرأي / التأكيد", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 8, closedTechnicalOpinion + "\n" + closedMomentumContextState + "\n" + filtersText, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = opinionTextColor, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardCompactTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 9, "الخطة", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 9, planText, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = planTextColor, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 10, "دخول / وقف", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 10, entryStopText, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = planActive ? planTextColor : neutralColor, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 0, 11, "الأهداف", width = dashboardLabelColumnWidth, text_color = color.white, bgcolor = labelBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
        table.cell(dashboardId, 1, 11, targetsText, width = dashboardValueColumnWidth, text_color = planActive ? planTextColor : neutralColor, bgcolor = valueBackground, text_size = dashboardRegularTextSize, text_halign = text.align_right)
    else
        table.clear(dashboardId, 0, 0, 1, 11)
    true

if barstate.islast
    f_render_dashboard(dashboard)

plot(planActive ? planEntry : na, "سعر دخول الخطة", display = display.none)
plot(planActive ? planStop : na, "وقف خسارة الخطة", display = display.none)
plot(planActive ? planTarget1 : na, "الهدف الأول", display = display.none)
plot(planActive ? planTarget2 : na, "الهدف الثاني", display = display.none)
plot(planActive ? planTarget3 : na, "الهدف الثالث", display = display.none)
plot(lastSwingHigh, "آخر قمة صحيحة", display = display.none)
plot(lastSwingLow, "آخر قاع صحيح", display = display.none)
plot(strongResistanceLevel, "المقاومة الأفقية القوية", display = display.none)
plot(strongSupportLevel, "الدعم الأفقي القوي", display = display.none)
plot(eventAlertLevel, "مستوى الحدث الفني", display = display.none)
plot(stopHitPrice, "سعر حدث الوقف", display = display.none)
plot(target1HitPrice, "سعر حدث الهدف الأول", display = display.none)
plot(target2HitPrice, "سعر حدث الهدف الثاني", display = display.none)
plot(target3HitPrice, "سعر حدث الهدف الثالث", display = display.none)

// Explicit alert invariants prevent malformed webhook payloads if future edits
// ever break the state-machine assumptions.
bool newPlanAlertValuesReady = newPlanCreatedEvent and planDirection != 0 and not na(planEntry) and not na(planStop) and not na(planTarget1) and not na(planTarget2) and not na(planTarget3)
bool stopAlertValueReady = stopHitEvent and not na(stopHitPrice)
bool target1AlertValueReady = target1HitEvent and not na(target1HitPrice)
bool target2AlertValueReady = target2HitEvent and not na(target2HitPrice)
bool target3AlertValueReady = target3HitEvent and not na(target3HitPrice)
bool wyckoffAlertValueReady = wyckoffEventNow and not na(wyckoffEventLevel)

alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and newPivotHigh, "قمة صحيحة جديدة", 'قمة محورية مؤكدة|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السعر={{plot("آخر قمة صحيحة")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and newPivotLow, "قاع صحيح جديد", 'قاع محوري مؤكد|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السعر={{plot("آخر قاع صحيح")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and strongResistanceBreak, "اختراق مقاومة أفقية قوية", 'اختراق مقاومة متعددة اللمسات|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("المقاومة الأفقية القوية")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and strongSupportBreak, "كسر دعم أفقي قوي", 'كسر دعم متعدد اللمسات|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("الدعم الأفقي القوي")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and (preliminarySupportEvent or sellingClimaxEvent), "وايكوف - دعم أولي أو ذروة بيع", 'تجميع وايكوف مرحلة A|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and (preliminarySupplyEvent or buyingClimaxEvent), "وايكوف - عرض أولي أو ذروة شراء", 'تصريف وايكوف مرحلة A|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and (accumulationAutomaticRallyEvent or distributionAutomaticReactionEvent or accumulationSecondaryTestEvent or distributionSecondaryTestEvent), "وايكوف - ارتداد تلقائي أو اختبار ثانوي", 'نطاق وايكوف AR أو ST|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and (accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent), "وايكوف تجميع - ربيع أو اختباره", 'تجميع وايكوف Spring/Test|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and (distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent), "وايكوف تصريف - UTAD أو اختباره", 'تصريف وايكوف UTAD/Test|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and (accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent), "وايكوف صاعد - إشارة قوة أو آخر دعم", 'تجميع وايكوف SOS/LPS|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and (distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent), "وايكوف هابط - علامة ضعف أو آخر عرض", 'تصريف وايكوف SOW/LPSY|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and twoSidedLiquiditySweep, "سحب سيولة من الجانبين", 'سحب سيولة محايد من الجانبين|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and ((bullishLiquiditySweep and not accumulationSpringEvent) or bearishBreakFailed), "سحب سيولة أو كسر وهمي صاعد", 'سحب سيولة صاعد|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and ((bearishLiquiditySweep and not distributionUtadEvent) or bullishBreakFailed), "سحب سيولة أو اختراق وهمي هابط", 'سحب سيولة هابط|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and bullishRetest, "إعادة اختبار صاعدة ناجحة", 'إعادة اختبار صاعدة|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and bearishRetest, "إعادة اختبار هابطة ناجحة", 'إعادة اختبار هابطة|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and bullishWyckoffTest and not accumulationSpringTestEvent, "اختبار سحب سيولة صاعد", 'اختبار سحب سيولة صاعد|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and bearishWyckoffTest and not distributionUtadTestEvent, "اختبار سحب سيولة هابط", 'اختبار سحب سيولة هابط|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and bullishDemandRetest, "اختبار منطقة طلب ناجح", 'اختبار طلب|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and bearishSupplyRetest, "اختبار منطقة عرض ناجح", 'اختبار عرض|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|المستوى={{plot("مستوى الحدث الفني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and bullishRsiDivergence, "دايفرجنس RSI صاعد مؤكد", 'دايفرجنس صاعد|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السعر={{plot("آخر قاع صحيح")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and bearishRsiDivergence, "دايفرجنس RSI هابط مؤكد", 'دايفرجنس هابط|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السعر={{plot("آخر قمة صحيحة")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and newPlanAlertValuesReady and planDirection == 1, "فرصة صعود السهم", 'فرصة صعود السهم|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|دخول={{plot("سعر دخول الخطة")}}|وقف={{plot("وقف خسارة الخطة")}}|هدف1={{plot("الهدف الأول")}}|هدف2={{plot("الهدف الثاني")}}|هدف3={{plot("الهدف الثالث")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and newPlanAlertValuesReady and planDirection == -1, "فرصة هبوط السهم", 'فرصة هبوط السهم|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|دخول={{plot("سعر دخول الخطة")}}|وقف={{plot("وقف خسارة الخطة")}}|هدف1={{plot("الهدف الأول")}}|هدف2={{plot("الهدف الثاني")}}|هدف3={{plot("الهدف الثالث")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and planInvalidatedEvent, "إلغاء الخطة بعد فشل الإعداد", 'إلغاء الخطة|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السبب=فشل الكسر أو تجاوز نقطة الإبطال')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and planReverseExitEvent, "إغلاق الخطة بإشارة عكسية", 'إغلاق الخطة بسبب إشارة عكسية|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and trailingStopRaisedEvent, "تحديث وقف حماية الأرباح", 'رفع وقف الأرباح المتحرك|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|الوقف={{plot("وقف خسارة الخطة")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and stopAlertValueReady, "ضرب الوقف عند اللمس", 'وقف عند اللمس|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السعر={{plot("سعر حدث الوقف")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and target1AlertValueReady, "تحقق الهدف 1 عند اللمس", 'هدف1 عند اللمس|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السعر={{plot("سعر حدث الهدف الأول")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and target2AlertValueReady, "تحقق الهدف 2 عند اللمس", 'هدف2 عند اللمس|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السعر={{plot("سعر حدث الهدف الثاني")}}')
alertcondition(stockChartAllowed and target3AlertValueReady, "تحقق الهدف 3 عند اللمس", 'هدف3 عند اللمس|{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|السعر={{plot("سعر حدث الهدف الثالث")}}')

f_send_dynamic_alerts() =>
    if dynamicAlerts and stockChartAllowed
        // Dynamic text must be escaped before insertion into JSON. Numeric fields
        // use f_json_number() so unavailable values become the valid literal null.
        string jsonTicker = f_json_escape(syminfo.tickerid)
        string jsonTimeframe = f_json_escape(timeframe.period)
        string jsonTradeText = f_json_escape(planTradeText)
        string jsonTargetHorizon = f_json_escape(planTargetHorizon)
        string jsonSetupSource = f_json_escape(planSetupSource)
        string jsonPriceContext = f_json_escape(planPriceContext)
        string jsonWyckoffEvent = f_json_escape(wyckoffEventText)
        string jsonWyckoffPhase = f_json_escape(wyckoffPhaseState)
        string jsonHigherTimeframe = f_json_escape(higherTimeframe)
        string reverseEventSuffix = planReverseExitEvent ? ',"إغلاق_عكسي":true' : ""
        string newPlanEventSuffix = newPlanAlertValuesReady ? ',"خطة_جديدة":"' + jsonTradeText + '","دخول_جديد":' + f_json_number(planEntry) + ',"وقف_جديد":' + f_json_number(planStop) + ',"أفق_الأهداف":"' + jsonTargetHorizon + '","توافق_جديد":' + str.tostring(planScore) + ',"من":' + str.tostring(planScoreMax) : ""
        string trailingStopEventSuffix = trailingStopRaisedEvent and not na(planStop) ? ',"وقف_محدث":' + f_json_number(planStop) : ""
        string transitionEventSuffix = reverseEventSuffix + newPlanEventSuffix + trailingStopEventSuffix
        // Preserve simultaneous analytical information in the same JSON message.
        // One alert call per execution remains efficient, while a new plan, stop,
        // target or Wyckoff event can no longer hide the other same-bar events.
        string wyckoffCompanionSuffix = wyckoffAlertValueReady ? ',"وايكوف_مصاحب":{"الحدث":"' + jsonWyckoffEvent + '","المرحلة":"' + jsonWyckoffPhase + '","المستوى":' + f_json_number(wyckoffEventLevel) + '}' : ""
        string failedBreakCompanionSuffix = bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed ? ',"كسر_وهمي_مصاحب":true' : ""
        string sweepCompanionSuffix = bullishSweep or bearishSweep ? ',"سحب_سيولة_مصاحب":true' : ""
        string analyticalEventSuffix = wyckoffCompanionSuffix + failedBreakCompanionSuffix + sweepCompanionSuffix
        if stopAlertValueReady
            string stopGapSuffix = stopGapFillEvent ? ',"تنفيذ_فجوة":true,"الوقف_المخطط":' + f_json_number(planStop) : ',"تنفيذ_فجوة":false'
            string stopTouchOrder = stopWithUnorderedTargetEvent ? "غير محسوم؛ أولوية الوقف" : stopAfterTargetSameBarEvent ? "هدف مسجل قبل الوقف" : ""
            string stopAmbiguitySuffix = stopAndTargetSameBarEvent ? ',"لمس_وقف_وهدف_بنفس_الشمعة":true,"ترتيب_اللمس":"' + f_json_escape(stopTouchOrder) + '"' : ',"لمس_وقف_وهدف_بنفس_الشمعة":false'
            string stopPrioritySuffix = stopWithUnorderedTargetEvent ? ',"الأولوية":"الوقف"' : ""
            string stopTrailSuffix = planTrailingStopActive ? ',"وقف_حماية_أرباح":true' : ',"وقف_حماية_أرباح":false'
            alert('{"الحدث":"وقف عند اللمس","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","السعر":' + f_json_number(stopHitPrice) + stopGapSuffix + stopAmbiguitySuffix + stopPrioritySuffix + stopTrailSuffix + transitionEventSuffix + analyticalEventSuffix + '}', alert.freq_all)
        else if target3AlertValueReady
            alert('{"الحدث":"هدف3 عند اللمس","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","السعر":' + f_json_number(target3HitPrice) + transitionEventSuffix + analyticalEventSuffix + '}', alert.freq_all)
        else if target2AlertValueReady
            alert('{"الحدث":"هدف2 عند اللمس","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","السعر":' + f_json_number(target2HitPrice) + transitionEventSuffix + analyticalEventSuffix + '}', alert.freq_all)
        else if target1AlertValueReady
            alert('{"الحدث":"هدف1 عند اللمس","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","السعر":' + f_json_number(target1HitPrice) + transitionEventSuffix + analyticalEventSuffix + '}', alert.freq_all)
        else if barstate.isconfirmed
            if planInvalidatedEvent
                alert('{"الحدث":"إلغاء الخطة بعد فشل الإعداد","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","الفاصل":"' + jsonTimeframe + '"' + transitionEventSuffix + analyticalEventSuffix + '}', alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
            else if planReverseExitEvent
                alert('{"الحدث":"إغلاق الخطة بسبب إشارة عكسية","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","الفاصل":"' + jsonTimeframe + '"' + newPlanEventSuffix + trailingStopEventSuffix + analyticalEventSuffix + '}', alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
            else if newPlanAlertValuesReady
                string counterTrendMessage = planCounterTrend ? ',"تحذير":"الخطة عكس اتجاه الإطار ' + jsonHigherTimeframe + '"' : ""
                string setupMessage = '{"الحدث":"' + jsonTradeText + '","المصدر":"' + jsonSetupSource + '","نوع_السعر":"' + jsonPriceContext + '","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","الفاصل":"' + jsonTimeframe + '","أفق_الأهداف":"' + jsonTargetHorizon + '","الدخول":' + f_json_number(planEntry) + ',"الوقف":' + f_json_number(planStop) + ',"هدف1":' + f_json_number(planTarget1) + ',"هدف2":' + f_json_number(planTarget2) + ',"هدف3":' + f_json_number(planTarget3) + ',"التوافق":' + str.tostring(planScore) + ',"من":' + str.tostring(planScoreMax) + counterTrendMessage + analyticalEventSuffix + '}'
                alert(setupMessage, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
            else if trailingStopRaisedEvent and not na(planStop)
                alert('{"الحدث":"تحديث وقف حماية الأرباح","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","الفاصل":"' + jsonTimeframe + '","الوقف":' + f_json_number(planStop) + analyticalEventSuffix + '}', alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
            else if wyckoffAlertValueReady
                alert('{"الحدث":"' + jsonWyckoffEvent + '","المرحلة":"' + jsonWyckoffPhase + '","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","الفاصل":"' + jsonTimeframe + '","المستوى":' + f_json_number(wyckoffEventLevel) + failedBreakCompanionSuffix + sweepCompanionSuffix + '}', alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
            else if bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed
                alert('{"الحدث":"كسر وهمي","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","الفاصل":"' + jsonTimeframe + '"}', alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
            else if bullishSweep or bearishSweep
                alert('{"الحدث":"سحب سيولة أو كسر وهمي","الرمز":"' + jsonTicker + '","الفاصل":"' + jsonTimeframe + '"}', alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
    true

f_send_dynamic_alerts()
